\displaystyle\begin{aligned} K_{22,2}&=\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{l}^{i}(A^{\kappa})_{l}^{k}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}(A^{\kappa})_{\mu}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi\partial_{t}^{5}\partial_{i}\phi}_{K_{22,21}}\\
&\underbrace{-\sum_{s=1}^{3}C_{3}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{l}^{i}\partial_{t}^{s}((A^{\kappa})_{l}^{k}(A^{\kappa})_{\mu}^{j})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\partial_{j}\phi\partial_{t}^{5}\partial_{i}\phi}_{K_{22,22}}.\end{aligned}

(4.78)

Integrating by part in time,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} K_{22,22}&=-\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{l}^{i}\partial_{j}\phi\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{l}^{k}(A^{\kappa})_{\mu}^{j})\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{i}\phi|_{0}^{t}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{l}^{i}\partial_{j}\phi)\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{l}^{k}(A^{\kappa})_{\mu}^{j})\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{i}\phi\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{l}^{i}\partial_{j}\phi\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{l}^{k}(A^{\kappa})_{\mu}^{j})\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{\mu})\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{i}\phi.\end{aligned}

(4.79)

Consequently,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |K_{22,22}|&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\|J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{l}^{i}\partial_{j}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{l}^{k}(A^{\kappa})_{\mu}^{j})\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{i}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{l}^{i}\partial_{j}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{l}^{k}(A^{\kappa})_{\mu}^{j})\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{i}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{l}^{i}\partial_{j}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{l}^{k}(A^{\kappa})_{\mu}^{j})\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{\mu})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{i}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi\|_{1}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(4.80)

Combining (
4.71
), (
4.74
), (
4.75
), (
4.77
), (
4.78
), (
4.80
), we can conclude that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{\mu}\partial_{t}^{5}\phi}_{K_{22,11}}\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{\mu}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{5}\phi}_{K_{22,21}}\\
&=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi|^{2}-\int_{\Omega}\frac{1}{2}J_{\kappa}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{j}\phi\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{i}\phi+\mathcal{R},\end{aligned}

(4.81)

where
|\mathcal{R}|\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi\|_{1}^{2}+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))
. Now, taking
\partial_{t}^{4}
on (
4.14
) again, testing
J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi
, and integrating by part, we can see that

\displaystyle\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{5}\phi\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi}_{J_{1}}+\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}\phi)\partial_{i}(J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi)}_{J_{2}}=\mathcal{P}_{0}\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}f\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi}_{J_{3}}

(4.82)

We can estimate
J_{1}
and
J_{3}
in a direct way:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |J_{1}|&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\|J_{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty(\Omega)}}\|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})),\end{aligned}

(4.83)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |J_{3}|&\lesssim+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{4}f\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(4.84)

Now, the expansion of
J_{2}
can be written as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} J_{2}&=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}\phi)\partial_{i}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi)\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{l}}_{J_{21}}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{l}}_{J_{22}}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi)J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{l}}_{J_{23}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{4}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi)J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{l}}_{J_{24}},\end{aligned}

(4.85)

Using the order of
g_{\kappa}^{ij}
and
\partial\phi
again,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |J_{21}|&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{4}(g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}\phi)\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{i}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(4.86)

For
J_{24}
, integrating by part in time,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} J_{24}&=\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{4}^{s+1}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi)J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}|_{0}^{t}\\
&-\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{4}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi)\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi)\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\\
&-\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{4}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi))\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}.\end{aligned}

(4.87)

Thus,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |J_{24}|&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{4}^{s+1}\|\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi)\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{4}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi)\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{4}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi))\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(4.88)

Now, we calculate the leading order of
J_{22}
:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} J_{22}&=\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\alpha}\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\beta}(A^{\kappa})_{\beta}^{i}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{l}}_{J_{22,1}}\\
&\underbrace{-\sum_{s=1}^{3}C_{3}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{s}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\alpha}(A^{\kappa})_{\beta}^{i})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{\alpha}v_{\kappa}^{\beta}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{l}}_{J_{22,2}},\end{aligned}

(4.89)

Also, estimate
J_{22,2}
via integrating by part in time,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} J_{22,2}&=-\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\int_{\Omega}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\alpha}(A^{\kappa})_{\beta}^{i})\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{\alpha}v_{\kappa}^{\beta}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}|_{0}^{t}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi)\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\alpha}(A^{\kappa})_{\beta}^{i})\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{\alpha}v_{\kappa}^{\beta}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\alpha}(A^{\kappa})_{\beta}^{i})\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{\alpha}v_{\kappa}^{\beta})\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}.\end{aligned}

(4.90)

Hence,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |J_{22,2}|&\lesssim\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\|\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\alpha}(A^{\kappa})_{\beta}^{i})\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{\alpha}v_{\kappa}^{\beta}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\alpha}(A^{\kappa})_{\beta}^{i})\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{\alpha}v_{\kappa}^{\beta}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\alpha}(A^{\kappa})_{\beta}^{i})\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{\alpha}v_{\kappa}^{\beta})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(4.91)

Now, combining (
4.83
), (
4.84
), (
4.85
),(
4.86
), (
4.88
), (
4.89
), (
4.91
). We can reformulate (
4.82
) as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi)J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{l}}_{-J_{23}}\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\alpha}\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\beta}(A^{\kappa})_{\beta}^{i}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{l}}_{-J_{22,1}}=\mathcal{R},\end{aligned}

(4.92)

where
|\mathcal{R}|\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi\|_{1}^{2}+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))
.
Now, adding (
4.81
) on (
4.92
), it yields that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{\mu}\partial_{t}^{5}\phi}_{K_{22,11}}\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{\mu}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{5}\phi}_{K_{22,21}}\\
&-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi)J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{l}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\alpha}\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\beta}(A^{\kappa})_{\beta}^{i}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{l}\\
=&-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{j}\phi\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{i}\phi+\mathcal{R},\end{aligned}

(4.93)

where
|\mathcal{R}|\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi\|_{1}^{2}+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))
.
Notice that the LHS of (
4.93
) can be written as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{\mu}\partial_{t}^{5}\phi+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\beta}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{\beta}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{l}}_{L_{1}}\\
\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{\mu}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{5}\phi-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi)J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{l}}_{L_{2}}\end{aligned}

(4.94)

Notice that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} L_{1}&=\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{t}^{5}\nabla^{\kappa}_{\mu}\phi}_{L_{11}}\underbrace{+\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{5}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{t}^{s}(A^{\kappa})_{\mu}^{i}\partial_{t}^{5-s}\partial_{i}\phi}_{L_{12}}\\
&\underbrace{-\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{t}^{s}((A^{\kappa})_{\mu}^{j}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i})\partial_{t}^{4-s}\partial_{j}v_{\kappa}^{k}\partial_{i}\phi}_{L_{13}}.\end{aligned}

(4.95)

We can compute that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |L_{13}|&\lesssim\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{4}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{i}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{\mu}^{j}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{j}v_{\kappa}^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(4.96)

Considering
L_{12}

we can control it as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |L_{12}|&\lesssim\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{5}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{\mu}^{i}\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{t}\partial_{i}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(4.97)

The remainder of
L_{1}
,
L_{11}
contributes the key cancellation term in our article as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} L_{11}&=\underbrace{-\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{\mu}\phi|_{0}^{t}}_{L_{11,1}}\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{\mu}\phi}_{L_{11,2}}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{l}^{i}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi)\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{\mu}\phi}_{L_{11,3}}.\end{aligned}

(4.98)

We have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |L_{11,3}|&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{l}^{i}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{\mu}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(4.99)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} L_{11,1}&=\underbrace{-\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{\mu}\partial_{t}^{4}\phi|_{0}^{t}}_{L_{11,11}}\underbrace{-\sum_{s=1}^{3}C_{4}^{s}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{t}^{s}(A^{\kappa})_{\mu}^{i}\partial_{t}^{4-s}\partial_{i}\phi|_{0}^{t}}_{L_{11,12}}\\
&\underbrace{-\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{t}^{4}(A^{\kappa})_{\mu}^{i}\partial_{i}\phi|_{0}^{t}}_{L_{11,13}}.\end{aligned}

(4.100)

From direct calculation,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} L_{11,13}&=\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi(A^{\kappa})_{\mu}^{j}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{j}v_{\kappa}^{k}\partial_{i}\phi|_{0}^{t}\\
&+\sum_{s=1}^{3}C_{3}^{s}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{t}^{s}((A^{\kappa})_{\mu}^{j}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{j}v_{\kappa}^{k}\partial_{i}\phi|_{0}^{t}.\end{aligned}

(4.101)

A standard
L^{2}
estimate shows that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |L_{11,13}|&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+\|J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{l}^{\nu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi(A^{\kappa})_{\mu}^{j}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\partial_{i}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{\nu}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{j}v_{\kappa}^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\|J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{l}^{\nu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{i}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{\nu}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{\mu}^{j}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i})\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{j}v_{\kappa}^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1}(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(4.102)

Invoking Sobolev interpolation theorem,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1.5}^{\frac{2}{3}},\end{aligned}

(4.103)

and Young’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |L_{11,13}|&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1.5}^{\frac{4}{3}}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1.5}^{2}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\\
&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1.5}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(4.104)

Similarly,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |L_{11,12}|&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{4}^{s+1}\|J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{l}^{\nu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{\nu}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{\mu}^{i}\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{i}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})),\end{aligned}

(4.105)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |L_{11,11}|&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{\Omega}\|J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{l}^{\nu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi(A^{\kappa})_{\mu}^{j}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{\nu}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{j}\partial_{t}^{4}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}+C(\delta)\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1}^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\\
&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1.5}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(4.106)

Now, combining (
4.100
), (
4.104
), (
4.105
) and (
4.106
), we have

\displaystyle|L_{11,1}|\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1.5}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(4.107)

Combining (
4.98
), (
4.99
) and (
4.107
), we have

\displaystyle L_{11}=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{\mu}\phi}_{L_{11,2}}+\mathcal{R}

(4.108)

where
|\mathcal{R}|\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1.5}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))
.
Combining (
4.95
), (
4.96
), (
4.97
) and (
4.108
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} L_{1}=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{\mu}\phi+\mathcal{R},\end{aligned}

(4.109)

where
|\mathcal{R}|\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1.5}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))
.
The
L_{2}
can be controlled directly:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} L_{2}&=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{\mu}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{5}\phi-\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi)J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}|_{0}^{t}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{5}(\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi)\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi)\partial_{t}^{4}(\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi)\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\\
&=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\partial_{t}^{5}\phi+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\partial_{t}^{5}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{s=1}^{5}C_{5}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{s}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\partial_{t}^{5-s}\partial_{i}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi}_{L_{21}}\\
&\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi)\partial_{t}^{4}(\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi)\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}}_{L_{22}}\underbrace{-\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi)J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}|_{0}^{t}}_{L_{23}}\end{aligned}

(4.110)

It suffices to control
L_{21}
-
L_{23}
term by term.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} L_{21}&=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{5}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\partial_{i}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi}_{L_{21,1}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{5}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\partial_{t}^{4-s}\partial_{i}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi}_{L_{21,2}},\end{aligned}

(4.111)

From a direct calculation

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |L_{21,2}|&\lesssim\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{5}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{t}\partial_{i}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(4.112)

We expand
L_{21,1}
as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} L_{21,1}&=\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\mu}\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{\mu}v_{\kappa}^{j}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{i}\partial_{i}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi}_{L_{21,11}}\\
&\underbrace{-\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{s}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\mu}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{i})\partial_{t}^{4-s}\partial_{\mu}v_{\kappa}^{j}\partial_{i}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi}_{L_{21,12}}.\end{aligned}

(4.113)

From a direct computation,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |L_{21,12}|&\lesssim\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{4}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|J_{\kappa}\partial_{i}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\mu}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{i})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{\mu}v_{\kappa}^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(4.114)

For
L_{21,11}
, integrating by part in time,
we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} L_{21,11}&=-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\mu}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\nu}\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{\mu}v_{\kappa}^{j}\partial_{\nu}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l})\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\\
&=-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\mu}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\nu}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{\mu}v_{\kappa}^{j}\partial_{\nu}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi|_{0}^{t}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\mu}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\nu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi)\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{\mu}v_{\kappa}^{j}\partial_{\nu}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\end{aligned}

(4.115)

We can deduce that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |L_{21,11}|&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+\frac{1}{2}\|J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\mu}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\nu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{\mu}v_{\kappa}^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{\nu}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\mu}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\nu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{\mu}v_{\kappa}^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{\nu}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\times\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}^{2}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\\
&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1.5}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(4.116)

Combining (
4.113
), (
4.114
) and (
4.116
), we have

\displaystyle|L_{21,1}|\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1.5}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(4.117)

Combining (
4.111
), (
4.112
), (
4.117
), we have

\displaystyle|L_{21}|\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1.5}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(4.118)

Now, we estimate
L_{22}
,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |L_{22}|&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}(\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi)\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(4.119)

and we use a similar strategy to express
L_{23}
as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} L_{23}&=-\int_{\Omega}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\mu}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{t}^{4}\phi J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\nu}\partial_{\nu}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}|_{0}^{t}-\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\mu}\partial_{\mu}\phi J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}|_{0}^{t}\\
&-\sum_{s=1}^{3}C_{4}^{s}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{s}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\mu}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{t}^{4-s}\phi J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\nu}\partial_{\nu}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}|_{0}^{t}\\
&=-\int_{\Omega}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\mu}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{t}^{4}\phi J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\nu}\partial_{\nu}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}|_{0}^{t}+\int_{\Omega}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{i}v_{\kappa}^{j}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{\mu})\partial_{\mu}\phi J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}|_{0}^{t}\\
&+\sum_{s=1}^{3}C_{3}^{s}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{s}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{\mu}))\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{i}v_{\kappa}^{j}\partial_{\mu}\phi J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}|_{0}^{t}\\
&-\sum_{s=1}^{3}C_{4}^{s}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{s}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\mu}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{t}^{4-s}\phi J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\nu}\partial_{\nu}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}|_{0}^{t}.\end{aligned}

(4.120)

Thus,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |L_{23}|&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+\|(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\mu}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\nu}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{\mu}\partial_{t}^{4}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{\nu}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\|(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{\mu}\partial_{\mu}\phi J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\nu}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{i}v_{\kappa}^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{\nu}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\|\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{\mu}))\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{i}v_{\kappa}^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{\mu}\phi J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\nu}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{\nu}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{4}^{s+1}\|\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\mu}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{t}^{3-s}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{\nu}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{\nu}\partial_{t}^{3}v_{\kappa}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1.5}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(4.121)

Combining (
4.110
), (
4.118
), (
4.119
) and (
4.121
), we have

\displaystyle|L_{2}|\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1.5}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(4.122)

Combining (
4.93
), (
4.109
), (
4.122
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{\mu}\phi=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{j}\phi\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{i}\phi+\mathcal{R},

(4.123)

where
|\mathcal{R}|\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi\|_{1}^{2}+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1.5}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))
.

Remark
.

This proposition will provide a key cancellation which is crucial in the full-time derivative estimate for
v
. For the estimate involving the spatial derivative for
\phi
, we can see that it is self-contained. And an
H^{-\frac{1}{2}}
-
H^{\frac{1}{2}}
duality may be employed to derive the rest of the estimate for
v
. Indeed, if we adjust our framework to start our estimate from
\|v_{ttt}\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}
instead of
\|v_{tttt}\|_{L^{2}}
, this cancellation will be much easier to obtain.

5
Energy estimate II: Estimate for
curl
and
div

5.1
Curl estimate for
v
,
v_{t}
,
v_{tt}
,
v_{ttt}
,

\eta

Proposition 5.1
.

\displaystyle\sup_{t}(\sum_{k=0}^{4}\|\text{curl }\partial_{t}^{k}\eta\|_{4.5-k}^{2}+\|\sqrt{\kappa}\text{curl }\eta\|_{5.5}^{2})+\sum_{k=0}^{4}\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\text{curl }\partial_{t}^{k}v\|_{4.5-k}^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(5.1)

Proof
.

The major difference arises from the necessity of estimate
\phi
in contrast to the methodology used in
[
10
]
. Our focus will be only on the modified segment of the proof.
Taking
\text{curl }_{\eta^{\kappa}}
on the second equation of (
3.15
) shows that

\displaystyle(\text{curl }_{\eta^{\kappa}}v_{t})^{i}=-\epsilon^{ijk}\nabla_{j}^{\kappa}\partial_{t}\phi^{\kappa}\cdot\nabla_{k}^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa},

(5.2)

Changing the sequence of
\partial_{t}
and
\text{curl }_{\eta^{\kappa}}
, we can arrive at

\displaystyle\partial_{t}(\text{curl }_{\eta^{\kappa}}v)^{i}+\epsilon^{inm}\nabla^{\kappa}_{n}(v^{\kappa})^{s}\nabla^{\kappa}_{s}v_{m}=-\epsilon^{ijk}\nabla_{j}^{\kappa}\partial_{t}\phi^{\kappa}\cdot\nabla_{k}^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa},

(5.3)

After integrating on time, we obtain

\displaystyle\text{curl }_{\eta^{\kappa}}v=\text{curl }u_{0}-\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon^{.nm}\nabla_{n}^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}\cdot\nabla^{\kappa}v_{m}-\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon^{.jk}\nabla_{j}^{\kappa}\partial_{t}\phi^{\kappa}\cdot\nabla_{k}^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}.

(5.4)

From (
5.4
), it is clear that

\displaystyle\text{curl }v+\epsilon^{.sw}\partial_{i}v_{w}\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{s}^{i}=\text{curl }u_{0}-\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon^{.nm}\nabla_{n}^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}\cdot\nabla^{\kappa}v_{m}-\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon^{.jk}\nabla_{j}^{\kappa}\partial_{t}\phi^{\kappa}\cdot\nabla_{k}^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}.

(5.5)

Given that the precise expression of the equation is not important, we can write

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \text{curl }v\sim\text{curl }u_{0}+\partial v\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}v+\int_{0}^{t}\nabla^{\kappa}\partial_{t}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\end{aligned}

(5.6)

Integrating on time again, it follows that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \text{curl }\eta&\sim t\text{curl }u_{0}+\int_{0}^{t}\partial v\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}v+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\nabla^{\kappa}\partial_{t}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\end{aligned}

(5.7)

Taking spatial derivative
D
, and integrating on time by part, we can write

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} D\text{curl }\eta&\sim tD\text{curl }u_{0}+\int_{0}^{t}\partial Dv\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}\partial v\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\partial_{t}DA^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}D\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}v+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}v\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}D(\nabla^{\kappa}\partial_{t}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa})\\
&\sim\underbrace{tD\text{curl }u_{0}+\partial D\eta\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}\partial D\eta\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}\partial vDA^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}A^{\kappa}DA^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa}Dv}_{I}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}D^{2}v^{\kappa}Dv+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa}D^{2}v}_{II}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}D(\nabla^{\kappa}\partial_{t}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa})}_{III}\end{aligned}

(5.8)

Notice that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}D(\nabla^{\kappa}\partial_{t}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa})&\sim\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}D(\partial_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa})+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}D(A^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa})\\
&\sim\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}D\partial_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\partial_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}A^{\kappa}D^{2}v^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}A^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}DA^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\\
&\sim\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}|_{0}^{t^{\prime}}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\partial_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}}_{III_{1}}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}DA^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}A^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}}_{III_{2}}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}A^{\kappa}D^{2}\eta^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}D^{2}\eta^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}A^{\kappa}D^{2}\eta^{\kappa}\partial_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}}_{III_{3}}\end{aligned}

(5.9)

We can control the term
III_{1}
,
III_{2}
and
III_{3}
is a direct way. Therefore,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|III\|_{3.5}&\lesssim t\mathcal{P}_{0}+(\int_{0}^{t}\|D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}(\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}+\int_{0}^{t}(\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|\partial_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}^{2})\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+\int_{0}^{t}(\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+\int_{0}^{t}(\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|\partial_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\\
&\lesssim T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(5.10)

The control of the terms corresponding to
\eta
(I and II) is the same as
[
10
]
, which reads

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|I\|_{3.5}&\lesssim t\|D\text{curl }u_{0}\|_{3.5}+\|\partial D\eta\|_{3.5}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}\|_{2.5}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial D\eta\|_{3.5}\|\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial v\|_{3.5}\|DA^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|A^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\|DA^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\|Dv^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\|Dv\|_{3.5}\\
&\lesssim T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(5.11)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} D^{2}(II)&\sim\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}D^{4}v^{\kappa}Dv+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa}D^{4}v}_{II_{2,1}}+\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}D^{3}v^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}D^{2}v+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}D^{3}vA^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}D^{2}v^{\kappa}}_{II_{2,2}}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}D^{3}v^{\kappa}D(A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa})Dv+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}Dv^{\kappa}D(A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa})D^{3}v}_{II_{2,3}}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}D^{2}v^{\kappa}D^{2}(A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa})Dv+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}D^{2}(A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa})Dv^{\kappa}D^{2}v+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}D^{2}v^{\kappa}D(A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa})D^{2}v}_{II_{2,4}},\end{aligned}

(5.12)

and integrating by part on time;

\displaystyle II_{2,1}=\int_{0}^{t}A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}D^{4}\eta^{\kappa}Dv+\int_{0}^{t}A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa}D^{4}\eta+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}D^{4}\eta^{\kappa}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}Dv)+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}D^{4}\eta\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa})

(5.13)

From a direct computation,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|II_{2,1}\|_{1.5}&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\|D^{4}\eta^{\kappa}\|_{1.5}\|A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}Dv\|_{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa}\|_{2}\|D^{4}\eta\|_{1.5}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|D^{4}\eta^{\kappa}\|_{1.5}\|\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}Dv)\|_{1.5}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|D^{4}\eta\|_{1.5}\|\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa})\|_{2}\\
&\lesssim T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(5.14)

The rest of terms of
II
are lower order,
\|II_{2,2}+II_{2,3}+II_{2,4}\|_{1.5}\lesssim T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})
.
Immediately, we obtain

\displaystyle\|D\text{curl }\eta\|_{3.5}\lesssim T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(5.15)

We can conclude that

\displaystyle\|\text{curl }\eta\|_{4.5}^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(5.16)

Now we focus on the estimate of
v
and the time derivatives on
v
.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \text{curl }v\sim\text{curl }u_{0}+\partial v\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}v+\int_{0}^{t}\nabla^{\kappa}\partial_{t}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa},\end{aligned}

(5.17)

\displaystyle\text{curl }v_{t}\sim\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}v+\partial_{t}\partial\phi^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}+\partial v_{t}\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}A^{\kappa},

(5.18)

\displaystyle\text{curl }v_{tt}\sim\nabla^{\kappa}v_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}+\partial_{t}(\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}v)+\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}\partial\phi^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa})+\partial v_{tt}\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}A^{\kappa},

(5.19)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \text{curl }v_{ttt}&\sim\nabla^{\kappa}v_{tt}\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}+\partial_{t}(\nabla^{\kappa}v_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa})+\partial_{t}^{2}(\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}v)+\partial_{t}^{2}(\partial_{t}\partial\phi^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa})+\partial v_{ttt}\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}.\end{aligned}

(5.20)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \text{curl }v_{tttt}&\sim\partial v_{ttt}\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}+\partial_{t}(\nabla^{\kappa}v_{tt}\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa})+\partial_{t}^{2}(\nabla^{\kappa}v_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa})+\partial_{t}^{3}(\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}v)\\
&+\partial_{t}^{3}(\partial_{t}\partial\phi^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa})+\partial v_{tttt}\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}.\end{aligned}

(5.21)

Roughly speaking, the time-spatial mixed norms of
\phi
are of order 4.5 in
L_{t}^{\infty}
and of order 5.5 in
L_{t}^{2}
. Consequently, the term involving
\phi^{\kappa}
is controlled by the established estimate of
\phi
. The term related to
v
is handled in the same manner as in
[
10
]
. Therefore, we present the estimate below without further elaboration.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\text{curl }v\|_{3.5}&\lesssim\|\text{curl }u_{0}\|_{3.5}+\|\partial v\|_{3.5}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}v\|_{3.5}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial\partial_{t}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\|A^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\\
&\lesssim\|\text{curl }u_{0}\|_{3.5}+\|\partial v\|_{3.5}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}v\|_{3.5}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial\partial_{t}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|A^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}^{2}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(5.22)

\displaystyle\|\text{curl }v_{t}\|_{2.5}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}v\|_{2.5}+\|\partial_{t}\partial\phi^{\kappa}\|_{2.5}\|A^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{2.5}+\|\partial v_{t}\|_{2.5}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}\|_{2.5}

(5.23)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),

(5.24)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\text{curl }v_{tt}\|_{1.5}&\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\partial v\|_{1.5}\|A^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}\|_{2}+\|\partial_{t}(\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}v)\|_{1.5}+\|\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}\partial\phi^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa})\|_{1.5}+\|\partial v_{tt}\|_{1.5}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}\|_{2}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(5.25)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\text{curl }v_{ttt}\|_{0.5}&\lesssim\|\partial_{t}^{2}\partial v\|_{0.5}\|A^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}\|_{2}+\|\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}\partial vA^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa})\|_{0.5}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}(\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}v)\|_{0.5}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}^{2}(\partial_{t}\partial\phi^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa})\|_{0.5}+\|\partial v_{ttt}\|_{0.5}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}\|_{2}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(5.26)

We now turn to controlling the terms involving
\kappa
. It is evident that the terms associated with
\phi
are better controlled for a similar reason. To be precise,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\sqrt{\kappa}D\text{curl }\eta\|_{4.5}\lesssim\|\sqrt{\kappa}I\|_{4.5}+\|\sqrt{\kappa}II\|_{4.5}+\|\sqrt{\kappa}III\|_{4.5},\end{aligned}

(5.27)

As

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} D^{2}(III_{1})&\sim\int_{0}^{t}D^{3}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}D^{2}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}D^{2}(D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}(0))\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}D^{3}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}+D^{2}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}D^{2}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}|_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\end{aligned}

(5.28)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\sqrt{\kappa}D^{2}(III_{1})\|_{2.5}&\lesssim T\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}D^{3}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{2.5}\|\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{2.5}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}D^{2}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{2.5}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|\sqrt{\kappa}D^{3}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{2.5}\|\partial_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{2.5}+\|\sqrt{\kappa}D^{2}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{2.5}\\
&\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(5.29)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} DIII_{2}&\sim\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}D^{2}A^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}DA^{\kappa}D^{2}v^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}DA^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}A^{\kappa}D^{2}v^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}A^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa}D^{2}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}A^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa},\end{aligned}

(5.30)

we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\sqrt{\kappa}DIII_{2}\|_{3.5}&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|\sqrt{\kappa}D^{2}A^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\|Dv^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|\sqrt{\kappa}D^{2}v^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\|DA^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|\sqrt{\kappa}DA^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|\sqrt{\kappa}D^{2}v^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\|A^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|\sqrt{\kappa}D^{2}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\|A^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|\sqrt{\kappa}A^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\\
&\lesssim T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(5.31)

as an application of Cauchy inequality.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} DIII_{3}&\sim\int_{0}^{t}A^{\kappa}D^{3}\eta^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}DA^{\kappa}D^{2}\eta^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}A^{\kappa}D^{2}\eta^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}D^{3}\eta^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}D^{2}v^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}D^{2}\eta^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}Dv^{\kappa}DA^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}D^{2}\eta^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}D^{2}\eta^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}A^{\kappa}D^{3}\eta^{\kappa}\partial_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}DA^{\kappa}D^{2}\eta^{\kappa}\partial_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}A^{\kappa}D^{2}\eta^{\kappa}\partial_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}A^{\kappa}D^{2}\eta^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}|_{0}^{t^{\prime}}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}A^{\kappa}D^{2}v^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}D^{2}\eta^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(5.32)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\sqrt{\kappa}DIII_{3}\|_{3.5}&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}D^{3}\eta^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\|A^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}DA^{\kappa}D^{2}\eta^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}A^{\kappa}D^{2}\eta^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|\sqrt{\kappa}D^{3}\eta^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|\sqrt{\kappa}D^{2}v^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\|A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}D^{2}\eta^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|\sqrt{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa}DA^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}D^{2}\eta^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}D^{2}\eta^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|\sqrt{\kappa}D^{3}\eta^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\|A^{\kappa}\partial_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|\sqrt{\kappa}DA^{\kappa}D^{2}\eta^{\kappa}\partial_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|\sqrt{\kappa}A^{\kappa}D^{2}\eta^{\kappa}\partial_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|\sqrt{\kappa}D^{2}v^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\|A^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|\sqrt{\kappa}D^{2}\eta^{\kappa}D\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa})\|_{3.5}+T\mathcal{P}_{0}\\
&\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(5.33)

invoking the Cauchy’s inequality. The estimate of
\|\sqrt{\kappa}I\|_{4.5}
and
\|\sqrt{\kappa}II\|_{4.5}
is the same as
[
10
]
. We omit the details.
Hence, we can write that
\|\sqrt{\kappa}\text{curl }\eta\|_{5.5}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})
.

Due to a similar reason, we can also control the terms related to
\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\text{curl }\partial_{t}^{k}v\|_{4.5-k}^{2}
,
k=0,1,2,3,4
.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} D\text{curl }v&\sim D\text{curl }u_{0}+D^{2}v\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}+Dv\int_{0}^{t}(D^{2}v^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}+Dv^{\kappa}DA^{\kappa}A^{\kappa})+D^{2}\phi^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}D\phi^{\kappa}|_{0}^{t}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}D^{2}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa})D\phi^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}D^{2}\phi^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}\partial_{t}D\phi^{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}D\partial_{t}\phi^{\kappa}D(A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa})D\phi^{\kappa},\end{aligned}

(5.34)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\sqrt{\kappa}D\text{curl }v\|_{3.5}&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+\|\sqrt{\kappa}D^{2}v\|_{3.5}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\\
&+\|Dv\|_{3.5}\int_{0}^{t}(\|\sqrt{\kappa}D^{2}v^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\|A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}+\sqrt{\kappa}\|Dv^{\kappa}DA^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}\|_{3.5})\\
&+\|\sqrt{\kappa}D^{2}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}(\|A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}D\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5})+\sqrt{\kappa}\int_{0}^{t}\|D^{2}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\|\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa})D\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\\
&+\sqrt{\kappa}\int_{0}^{t}\|D^{2}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\|A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\|\partial_{t}D\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}+\sqrt{\kappa}\int_{0}^{t}\|D\partial_{t}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\|D(A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa})D\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\\
&\lesssim\|\sqrt{\kappa}D^{2}v\|_{3.5}T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(5.35)

We obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\text{curl }v\|_{4.5}^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(5.36)

Similarly,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\sqrt{\kappa}\text{curl }v_{t}\|_{3.5}&\lesssim\sqrt{\kappa}\|A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa}Dv\|_{3.5}+\sqrt{\kappa}\|\partial_{t}\partial\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\|A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}D\phi^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}+\|\sqrt{\kappa}Dv_{t}\|_{3.5}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}\|_{3.5}\\
&\lesssim\sqrt{\kappa}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+\|\sqrt{\kappa}Dv_{t}\|_{3.5}(T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})),\end{aligned}

(5.37)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\text{curl }v_{t}\|_{3.5}^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(5.38)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\sqrt{\kappa}\text{curl }v_{tt}\|_{2.5}&\lesssim\sqrt{\kappa}\|Dv_{t}A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa}\|_{2.5}+\sqrt{\kappa}\|\partial_{t}(\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}v)\|_{2.5}+\sqrt{\kappa}\|\partial_{t}^{2}D\phi^{\kappa}\|_{2.5}\|A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}D\phi^{\kappa}\|_{2.5}\\
&+\sqrt{\kappa}\|\partial_{t}D\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}D\phi^{\kappa})\|_{2.5}+\|\sqrt{\kappa}Dv_{tt}\|_{2.5}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}\|_{2.5}\\
&\lesssim\sqrt{\kappa}(\mathcal{P}_{0}+\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))+\|\sqrt{\kappa}Dv_{tt}\|_{2.5}T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(5.39)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\text{curl }v_{tt}\|_{2.5}^{2}&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(5.40)

,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\sqrt{\kappa}\text{curl }v_{ttt}\|_{1.5}&\lesssim\sqrt{\kappa}\|\partial_{t}^{2}DvA^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa}\|_{1.5}+\sqrt{\kappa}\|\partial_{t}(Dv_{t}A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa})\|_{1.5}\\
&+\sqrt{\kappa}\|\partial_{t}^{2}(\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}v)\|_{0.5}+\sqrt{\kappa}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial\phi^{\kappa}\|_{1.5}\|A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}D\phi^{\kappa}\|_{2}\\
&+\sqrt{\kappa}\sum_{i+j=1}\|\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{t}\partial\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{j}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}D\phi^{\kappa})\|_{1.5}+\sqrt{\kappa}\|Dv_{ttt}\|_{1.5}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}\|_{2}\\
&\lesssim\sqrt{\kappa}(\mathcal{P}_{0}+\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))+\sqrt{\kappa}\|Dv_{ttt}\|_{1.5}T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(5.41)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\text{curl }v_{ttt}\|_{1.5}^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),

(5.42)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\sqrt{\kappa}\text{curl }v_{tttt}\|_{0.5}&\lesssim\sqrt{\kappa}(\|Dv_{ttt}\|_{0.5}\|\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}\|_{2}+\|\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}Dv_{t}A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa})\|_{0.5})\\
&+\sqrt{\kappa}(\|\partial_{t}^{2}(Dv_{t}A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}Dv^{\kappa})\|_{0.5}+\|\partial_{t}^{3}(\nabla^{\kappa}v^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}v)\|_{0.5})\\
&+\sqrt{\kappa}\|\partial_{t}^{4}D\phi^{\kappa}\|_{0.5}\|A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}D\phi^{\kappa}\|_{2}+\sqrt{\kappa}\sum_{i+j=2}\|\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{t}D\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{j}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa}D\phi^{\kappa})\|_{0.5}\\
&+\|\sqrt{\kappa}Dv_{tttt}\|_{0.5}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}A^{\kappa}\|_{2}\\
&\lesssim\sqrt{\kappa}(\mathcal{P}_{0}+\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))+\sqrt{\kappa}\|\partial_{t}^{4}D\phi^{\kappa}\|_{0.5}(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))+\|\sqrt{\kappa}v_{tttt}\|_{1.5}(T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})),\end{aligned}

(5.43)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\text{curl }v_{tttt}\|_{0.5}^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(5.44)

This completes the curl estimate (
5.1
).

5.2
Div estimate

Proposition 5.2
.

\displaystyle\sup_{t}(\sum_{k=0}^{4}\|\text{div }\partial_{t}^{k}\eta\|_{4.5-k}^{2}+\|\sqrt{\kappa}\text{div }\eta\|_{5.5}^{2})+\sum_{k=0}^{4}\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\text{div }\partial_{t}^{k}v\|_{4.5-k}^{2}\lesssim\delta E^{\kappa}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(5.45)

Proof
.

Since the estimate is totally based on the incompressible condition, an identical argument in
[
10
]
is enough. i.e. No term is related
\phi
. (Actually, it is exactly the same estimate of terms without
\phi
as above). To see this,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j}D\partial_{j}\eta^{i})&=\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j}D\partial_{j}\eta^{i}+(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j}D\partial_{j}v^{i}\\
&=\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j}D\partial_{j}\eta^{i}-D(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j}\partial_{j}v^{i}.\end{aligned}

(5.46)

Thus,

\displaystyle D\text{div}\,\eta=D\partial_{j}\eta^{i}\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j}+\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j}D\partial_{j}\eta^{i}-\int_{0}^{t}D(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j}\partial_{j}v^{i}.

(5.47)

Now, other estimate is similar.

6
Energy estimate III: Estimate for
\bar{\partial}_{A}

We first establish the regularity of the pressure. For
\|q\|_{4.5}
,
\|q_{t}\|_{3.5}
, and
\|q_{tt}\|_{2.5}
, we apply the elliptic estimate provided in (
2.25
) under the Neumann boundary condition. Regarding the data
\|q\|_{L^{2}}
,
\|q_{t}\|_{L^{2}}
, and
\|q_{tt}\|_{L^{2}}
, the bounds obtained from a rough elliptic estimate under the Dirichlet boundary condition for
\|q\|_{H^{1}}
,
\|q_{t}\|_{H^{1}}
, and
\|q{tt}\|_{H^{1}}
are sufficient.

However, when estimating
\|q_{ttt}\|_{1}
, we require the additional assistance of
\bar{q}=\frac{1}{|\Omega|}\int q
to provide a sharper bound. If we were to rely only on the elliptic estimate with Dirichlet boundary conditions for
\|q_{ttt}\|_{1}
to control
\|q_{ttt}\|_{L^{2}}
, a regularity loss would occur at the boundary. Specifically,
\|\bar{\Delta}\partial_{t}^{3}\eta\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial\Omega)}\sim\|v_{tt}\|_{H^{2.5}(\partial\Omega)}
, while our energy functional only contains
\|\partial_{t}^{2}v\|_{H^{2.5}(\Omega)}
. This discrepancy highlights the need for a more refined approach. An appropriate form of lemma was proposed by Ignotova & Kukavica
[
38
]
stated as following:

Lemma 6.1

(Low regularity elliptic estimates)
.

Let
\Omega
be a smooth bounded domain.
\|b^{ij}\|_{\infty}\leq M
satisfies strong elliptic condition. i.e.
b^{ij}\xi_{i}\xi_{j}\geq c|\xi|^{2}
,
\forall\xi\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
for some constant
c>0
. Let
q
be an
H^{1}
weak solution of the

\displaystyle\partial_{i}(b^{ij}\partial_{j}q)

\displaystyle=\text{div}\,\pi\quad\textit{in $\Omega$,}

(6.1)

\displaystyle b_{mk}\partial_{k}qN^{m}

\displaystyle=g\quad\textit{on $\partial\Omega$,}

(6.2)

where
\pi
,
\text{div}\,\pi\in L^{2}(\Omega)
and
g\in H^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\Omega)
with the compatibility condition

\displaystyle\int_{\partial\Omega}(\pi\cdot N-g)=0,

(6.3)

then we have

\displaystyle\|q-\bar{q}\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}\lesssim\|\pi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\|g-\pi\cdot N\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\partial\Omega)}.

(6.4)

Proof
.

See
[
38
]
. Alternatively, we prove the lemma as follows:
First, we use the definition of weak solution.

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}b^{ij}\partial_{j}q\partial_{i}\phi=\int_{\partial\Omega}g\phi-\int_{\Omega}\text{div}\,\pi\phi,\quad\text{$\forall\phi\in H^{1}(\Omega)$.}

(6.5)

Since
\pi
,
\text{div}\,\pi\in L^{2}(\Omega)
, the normal trace of
\pi
is well defined. We can write

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}b^{ij}\partial_{j}q\partial_{i}\phi=\int_{\partial\Omega}g\phi+\int_{\Omega}\pi\cdot\nabla\phi-\int_{\partial\Omega}\pi\cdot N\phi.

(6.6)

Setting
\phi=q
and utilizing Sobolev trace lemma, we find

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\nabla q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}&\lesssim\|g-\pi\cdot N\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\partial\Omega)}\|q\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial\Omega)}+\|\pi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\nabla q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\|g-\pi\cdot N\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\partial\Omega)}\|q\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}+\|\pi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|q\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}.\end{aligned}

(6.7)

Since
q-\bar{q}=q-\frac{1}{|\Omega|}\int_{\Omega}q
is also a weak solution to (
6.1
), we get

\displaystyle\|\nabla(q-\bar{q})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}\lesssim(\|\pi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\|g-\pi\cdot N\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\partial\Omega)})\|q-\bar{q}\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}

(6.8)

Now, invoking Poincaré-Wirtinger inequality, we have

\displaystyle\|q-\bar{q}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\lesssim\|\nabla(q-\bar{q})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}.

(6.9)

Combining (
6.8
) and (
6.9
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|q-\bar{q}\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}\lesssim\|\pi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\|g-\pi\cdot N\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\partial\Omega)}.

(6.10)

Now, we study our case
\Omega=\mathbb{T}^{2}\times(0,1)
. Notice that

\displaystyle\|q\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}\leq\|q-\bar{q}\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}+|\bar{q}||\Omega|^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(6.11)

It remains to control
|\bar{q}|
. To this end, let
H
be a harmonic function satisfying

\displaystyle\Delta H

\displaystyle=1,\quad\text{in $\Omega$,}

(6.12)

\displaystyle H

\displaystyle=0,\quad\text{on $\Gamma_{1}$,}

(6.13)

\displaystyle\partial_{N}H

\displaystyle=1.\quad\text{on $\Gamma_{0}$}

(6.14)

Actually,
H=\frac{1}{2}x_{3}^{2}-1
. A direct computation shows that

\displaystyle|\bar{q}|=|\int_{\Omega}q\Delta H|=|-\int_{\Omega}\nabla q\nabla H+\int_{\Gamma_{1}}q|\leq\|\nabla q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\|q\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}.

(6.15)

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\|q\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}\lesssim\|\pi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\|g-\pi\cdot N\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\Omega)}+\|q\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}.

(6.16)

Remark
.

However, due to the existence of artificial viscosity term, for
q_{ttt}
, we can’t use Dirichlet data for
q_{ttt}
directly. Instead, we use Euler equation to give its Dirichlet boundary data.

Lemma 6.2

(Pressure estimate)
.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|q\|_{4.5}+\|q_{t}\|_{3.5}+\|q_{tt}\|_{2.5}\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\end{aligned}.

(6.17)

\displaystyle\|q_{ttt}\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}^{2}\lesssim(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\|v_{tttt}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\mathcal{P}(E_{\kappa}^{\prime})+\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})

(6.18)

Proof
.

Consider

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \partial_{i}(g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}q)&=-\partial_{i}(\phi^{\kappa}_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}\phi^{\kappa})-\partial_{i}((A^{\kappa})_{j}^{i}(\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa})\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa})-\partial_{i}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}v_{t}^{k})\quad\text{in $\Omega$,}\\
(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\partial_{i}q&=-\phi^{\kappa}_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi^{\kappa}-\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{3}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa}-(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}v_{t}^{k}\quad\text{on $\Gamma$},\\
q&=-\sigma(\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n_{\kappa})+\kappa\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}(1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\quad\textit{on $\Gamma_{1}$}.\end{aligned}

(6.19)

Utilizing Lemma 2.2, we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|q\|_{4.5}\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})(&\|-\partial_{i}(\phi^{\kappa}_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}\phi^{\kappa})-\partial_{i}((A^{\kappa})_{j}^{i}(\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa})\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa})-\partial_{i}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}v_{t}^{k})\|_{2.5}\\
&\|-\phi^{\kappa}_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi^{\kappa}-\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa}-(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}v_{t}^{k}\|_{H^{3}(\Gamma)}+\|q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}).\end{aligned}

(6.20)

Using the lower order estimate

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|q\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}&\lesssim\|\phi^{\kappa}_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\|A^{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|A^{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|v_{t}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\mathcal{P}(\|\eta\|_{H^{2}(\partial\Omega)},\kappa\|v\|_{H^{2}(\partial\Omega)},\|\partial\eta\|_{L^{\infty}(\partial\Omega)})\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.21)

It implies that

\displaystyle\|q\|_{4.5}\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.22)

Differentiating (
6.19
) on time, we find

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \partial_{i}(g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}q_{t})&=-\partial_{i}(\partial_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}q)+\partial_{t}(-\partial_{i}(\phi^{\kappa}_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}\phi^{\kappa})-\partial_{i}((A^{\kappa})_{j}^{i}(\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa})\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa})-\partial_{i}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}v_{t}^{k}))\quad\text{in $\Omega$,}\\
(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\partial_{i}q_{t}&=-\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i})\partial_{i}q-\partial_{t}(\phi^{\kappa}_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi^{\kappa}-\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{3}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa}-(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}v_{t}^{k})\quad\text{on $\Gamma$},\\
q_{t}&=-\sigma\partial_{t}(\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n)+\kappa\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}(1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n_{\kappa}))\quad\textit{on $\Gamma_{1}$}.\end{aligned}

(6.23)

Using the estimate of order, it is not difficult to find

\displaystyle\|-\partial_{i}(\partial_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}q)\|_{1.5}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\|_{2.5}\|\partial q\|_{2.5}\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),

(6.24)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}(-\partial_{i}(\phi^{\kappa}_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}\phi^{\kappa})-\partial_{i}((A^{\kappa})_{j}^{i}(\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa})\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa})-\partial_{i}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}v_{t}^{k}))\|_{1.5}\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),

(6.25)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\|-\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i})\partial_{i}q-\partial_{t}(\phi^{\kappa}_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi^{\kappa}-\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{3}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa}-(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}v_{t}^{k})\|_{H^{2}(\partial\Omega)}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial q\|_{2.5}\times\|\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa}A^{\kappa})\|_{2.5}+\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\\
\lesssim&\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.26)

\displaystyle\|-\partial_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}q-\partial_{t}((\phi^{\kappa}_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}\phi^{\kappa})-\partial_{i}((A^{\kappa})_{j}^{i}(\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa})\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa})-\partial_{i}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}v_{t}^{k}))\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),

(6.27)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|q_{t}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}&\lesssim\|-\sigma\partial_{t}(\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n)+\kappa\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}(1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}(\|v\|_{H^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\eta\|_{H^{2}(\Gamma_{1})},\|\partial\eta\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})},\|\partial v\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})})+\kappa\|\partial_{t}(v\cdot n)\|_{H^{2}(\partial\Omega)}\times\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.28)

After a similar argument, it gives us

\displaystyle\|q_{t}\|_{3.5}\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.29)

Differentiating (
6.23
) on time, we can find that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \partial_{i}(g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}q_{tt})&=-\partial_{i}(\partial_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}q_{t})-\partial_{i}\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}q)-\partial_{t}^{2}(\partial_{i}(\phi^{\kappa}_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}\phi^{\kappa})\\
&-\partial_{i}((A^{\kappa})_{j}^{i}(\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa})\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa})-\partial_{i}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}v_{t}^{k}))\quad\text{in $\Omega$,}\\
(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\partial_{i}q_{tt}&=-\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i})\partial_{i}q_{t}-\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i})\partial_{i}q)\\
&-\partial_{t}^{2}(\phi^{\kappa}_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi^{\kappa}-\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{3}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa}-(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}v_{t}^{k})\quad\text{on $\Gamma$},\\
q_{tt}&=-\sigma\partial_{t}^{2}(\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n)+\kappa\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}(1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\quad\textit{on $\Gamma_{1}$}.\end{aligned}

(6.30)

Applying a similar argument

\displaystyle\|q_{tt}\|_{2.5}\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.31)

Differentiating (
6.30
) on time, we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \partial_{i}(g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}q_{ttt})&=-\partial_{i}(\partial_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}q_{tt})-\partial_{i}\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}q_{t})-\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{2}(\partial_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}q)\\
&-\partial_{t}^{3}(\partial_{i}(\phi^{\kappa}_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}\phi^{\kappa})-\partial_{i}((A^{\kappa})_{j}^{i}(\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa})\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa})-\partial_{i}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}v_{t}^{k}))\quad\text{in $\Omega$,}\\
(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\partial_{i}q_{ttt}&=-\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i})\partial_{i}q_{tt}-\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i})\partial_{i}q_{t})-\partial_{t}^{2}(\partial_{t}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i})\partial_{i}q)\\
&-\partial_{t}^{3}(\phi^{\kappa}_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi^{\kappa}-\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{3}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa}-(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{3}v_{t}^{k})\quad\text{on $\Gamma$}.\end{aligned}

(6.32)

Observe that

\displaystyle g-\pi\cdot N=0,

(6.33)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\pi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}&\lesssim\|\partial_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}q_{tt}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\|\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}q_{t})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}(\partial_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}q)\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}^{3}(\phi^{\kappa}_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{j}\phi^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\|\partial_{t}^{3}((A^{\kappa})_{j}^{i}(\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa})\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\|\partial_{t}^{3}((A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}v_{t}^{k}))\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\|\partial_{t}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{j}q_{tt}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\|(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|v_{tttt}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\|\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\mathcal{P}(E_{\kappa}^{\prime})+\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.34)

Now, we come back to the Euler equation:

Taking
\partial_{t}^{3}

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{4}v_{i}+\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}q_{ttt}+\sum_{k=1}^{3}C_{3}^{k}\partial_{t}^{k}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j}\partial_{t}^{3-k}\partial_{j}q=-\partial_{t}^{3}(\phi^{\kappa}_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})-\partial_{t}^{3}(\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa})

(6.35)

Now, testing
x_{3}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\partial_{t}^{3}q
, (
x_{3}
itself is a periodic function)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \int_{\Omega}\frac{1}{2}x_{3}g_{\kappa}^{3j}\partial_{j}(|q_{ttt}|^{2})&=\int_{\Omega}-\partial_{t}^{4}v_{i}x_{3}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\partial_{t}^{3}q-\int_{\Omega}\sum_{k=1}^{3}C_{3}^{k}\partial_{t}^{k}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j}\partial_{t}^{3-k}\partial_{j}qx_{3}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\partial_{t}^{3}q\\
&-\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{3}(\phi^{\kappa}_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})x_{3}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\partial_{t}^{3}q-\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{3}(\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa})x_{3}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\partial_{t}^{3}q\end{aligned}

(6.36)

Integrating by part, we will have the desired boundary expression:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \int_{\Gamma_{1}}\frac{1}{2}g_{\kappa}^{33}|q_{ttt}|^{2}&=\int_{\Omega}\frac{1}{2}\partial_{j}(x_{3}g_{\kappa}^{3j})q_{ttt}+\int_{\Omega}-\partial_{t}^{4}v_{i}x_{3}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\partial_{t}^{3}q-\int_{\Omega}\sum_{k=1}^{3}C_{3}^{k}\partial_{t}^{k}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j}\partial_{t}^{3-k}\partial_{j}qx_{3}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\partial_{t}^{3}q\\
&-\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{3}(\phi^{\kappa}_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})x_{3}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\partial_{t}^{3}q-\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{3}(\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa})x_{3}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\partial_{t}^{3}q\end{aligned}

(6.37)

Now, following from our assumption on
g_{\kappa}^{33}
and Young’s inequality, it is not difficult to find

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|q_{ttt}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}&\lesssim\|\partial_{j}(x_{3}g_{\kappa}^{3j})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|q_{ttt}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\|\partial_{t}^{4}v_{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|x_{3}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j}x_{3}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\partial_{j}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}^{2}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j}x_{3}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}\partial_{j}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}^{3}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j}\|_{L^{3}(\Omega)}\|x_{3}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\partial_{j}q\|_{L^{6}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\|\partial_{t}^{3}(\phi^{\kappa}_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|x_{3}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}^{3}(\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{k}\phi^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|x_{3}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\delta\|q_{ttt}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}(E_{\kappa}^{\prime})+\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\\
&+C(\delta)(\|\partial_{t}^{4}v_{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}\|x_{3}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j}x_{3}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}^{2}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\partial_{j}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2})\\
&\lesssim\delta\|q_{ttt}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.38)

Thus,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|q_{ttt}\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}^{2}&\lesssim\|\pi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\|q_{ttt}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}\\
&\lesssim\delta\|q_{ttt}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\end{aligned}

(6.39)

Choosing appropriate small
\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\|q_{ttt}\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.40)

Now, thanks to
\|q_{tt}\|_{1}\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})
, and
\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})
, we rewrite

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|q_{ttt}\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}^{2}&\lesssim\|\pi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\|q_{ttt}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}\\
&\lesssim\|(A^{\kappa})_{k}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}^{2}\|v_{tttt}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\|q_{ttt}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)\|\partial_{t}^{4}v_{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}\|x_{3}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}^{2}\\
&+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}(E_{\kappa}^{\prime})+\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\end{aligned}

(6.41)

Choosing small
\delta
again, we have

\displaystyle\|q_{ttt}\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}^{2}\lesssim(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\|v_{tttt}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\mathcal{P}(E_{\kappa}^{\prime})+\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})

(6.42)

We also need the following lemma to control the term
n-n^{\kappa}
.

Lemma 6.3
.

\displaystyle\sup_{t}(\|n-n^{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}+\|\bar{\partial}n-\bar{\partial}n^{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}+\|\partial_{t}\bar{\partial}(n-n^{\kappa})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})})\lesssim\sqrt{\kappa}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.43)

\displaystyle\sup_{t}\sum_{k=2}^{3}\|\partial_{t}^{k}(n-n^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\lesssim\kappa\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),

(6.44)

\displaystyle\sup_{t}\|\partial_{t}^{4}(n-n^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\lesssim\kappa\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+\kappa\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}.

(6.45)

Proof
.

Recall

\displaystyle J(A)_{i}^{3}

\displaystyle=(\partial_{1}\eta_{2}\partial_{2}\eta_{3}-\partial_{2}\eta_{2}\partial_{1}\eta_{3},\partial_{2}\eta_{1}\partial_{1}\eta_{3}-\partial_{1}\eta_{1}\partial_{2}\eta_{3},\partial_{1}\eta_{1}\partial_{2}\eta_{2}-\partial_{2}\eta_{1}\partial_{1}\eta_{2}),

\displaystyle J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}

\displaystyle=(\partial_{1}\eta_{2}^{\kappa}\partial_{2}\eta_{3}^{\kappa}-\partial_{2}\eta_{2}^{\kappa}\partial_{1}\eta_{3}^{\kappa},\partial_{2}\eta_{1}^{\kappa}\partial_{1}\eta_{3}^{\kappa}-\partial_{1}\eta_{1}^{\kappa}\partial_{2}\eta_{3}^{\kappa},\partial_{1}\eta_{1}^{\kappa}\partial_{2}\eta_{2}^{\kappa}-\partial_{2}\eta_{1}^{\kappa}\partial_{1}\eta_{2}^{\kappa}).

Therefore, we can find a rational function
F
such that
J(A)_{i}^{3}=F(\partial\eta)
and
J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}=F(\partial\eta^{\kappa})
. Define
G(x):\mathbb{R}^{3}-\{0\}\to\mathbb{R}^{3}
by
G(x)=\frac{x}{|x|}
. Then
n=G(JA_{i}^{3})
and
n^{\kappa}=G(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} G(JA_{i}^{3})-G(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})&=\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{s}G(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}+s(J(A)_{i}^{3}-J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}))ds\\
&=\int_{0}^{1}DG(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}+s(J(A)_{i}^{3}-J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}))ds\times(J(A)_{i}^{3}-J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}))\\
