&=\int_{0}^{1}DG(J(A_{i}^{3})+s(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}-J(A)_{i}^{3}))ds\times\int_{0}^{1}DF(\bar{\partial}\eta^{\kappa}+h(\bar{\partial}\eta-\bar{\partial}\eta^{\kappa}))dh\times(\bar{\partial}\eta-\bar{\partial}\eta^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.46)

Thus, the boundary analysis using the property of the horizontal mollifier yields the desired estimate.

We now present the following list of boundary estimates:

Lemma 6.4
.

\displaystyle\sup_{t}\sum_{k=0}^{4}\|\partial_{t}^{k}n\|_{H^{4-k}(\Gamma_{1})}+\|\partial_{t}^{k}n^{\kappa}\|_{H^{4-k}(\Gamma_{1})}+\|\partial_{t}^{k}J_{\kappa}\|_{H^{4-k}(\Gamma_{1})}\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),

(6.47)

\displaystyle\sup_{t}\|\bar{\partial}\partial_{t}^{4}n^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}+\|\bar{\partial}\partial_{t}^{4}n\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{2}(\Gamma_{1})},

(6.48)

\displaystyle\sup_{t}\|\bar{\partial}^{2}\partial_{t}^{3}n^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}+\|\bar{\partial}^{2}\partial_{t}^{3}n\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\|\partial_{t}^{2}v\|_{H^{3}(\Gamma_{1})},

(6.49)

\displaystyle\sup_{t}\|n^{\kappa}\|_{H^{5}(\Gamma_{1})}+\|n\|_{H^{5}(\Gamma_{1})}\lesssim(\mathcal{P}_{0}+\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\|\eta\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})}+\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.50)

Proof
.

Notice that
n\sim\partial\eta
. We omit the details.

Now, as a consequence, we rewrite the estimate for
\|q_{ttt}\|_{1}
as

Corollary 6.5
.

\displaystyle\sup_{t}\|q_{ttt}\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+\kappa\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\times(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))+\kappa\|\partial_{t}^{2}v\|_{H^{3}(\Gamma_{1})}\times(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(6.51)

The following two lemmas provide the error analysis for the boundary coercivity term.

Lemma 6.6
.

\displaystyle\sup_{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}^{5}\eta^{3}-\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}^{5}\eta\cdot n_{\kappa})\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+\sum_{k=0}^{4}\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{k}\bar{\partial}^{4-k}v^{3}-\partial_{t}^{k}\bar{\partial}^{4-k}v\cdot n_{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}\lesssim T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.52)

Proof
.

The proof follows from the definition of
n_{\kappa}
.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}^{5}\eta^{3}\cdot\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}n_{\kappa})\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}&\lesssim\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}^{5}\eta\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}T\|\partial_{t}n_{\kappa}\|_{H^{1.5}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\end{aligned}

(6.53)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \sum_{k=0}^{4}\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}(\partial_{t}^{k}\bar{\partial}^{4-k}v^{3}-\partial_{t}^{k}\bar{\partial}^{4-k}v\cdot n_{\kappa})\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}&=\sum_{k=0}^{4}\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{k}\bar{\partial}^{4-k}v\cdot\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\partial_{t}n_{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}\\
&\lesssim\sum_{k=0}^{4}\int_{0}^{t}(\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{k}\bar{\partial}^{4-k}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}(\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\|\partial_{t}n_{\kappa}\|_{H^{1.5}(\Gamma_{1})})^{2})\\
&\lesssim\sum_{k=0}^{4}\int_{0}^{t}(\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{k}\bar{\partial}^{4-k}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\\
&\lesssim T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.54)

Lemma 6.7
.

\displaystyle\sup_{t}\|\bar{\partial}^{6}\eta^{\lambda}\Pi_{\lambda}^{3}-\bar{\partial}^{6}\eta^{3}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+\sum_{k=0}^{4}\|\partial_{t}^{k}\bar{\partial}^{5-k}\eta^{\lambda}\Pi_{\lambda}^{3}-\partial_{t}^{k}\bar{\partial}^{5-k}\eta^{3}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.55)

Proof
.

Notice that when
t=0
,
\bar{\partial}\eta^{3}=0
on
\Gamma_{1}
.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \sum_{k=0}^{4}\|\partial_{t}^{k}\bar{\partial}^{5-k}\eta^{\lambda}\Pi_{\lambda}^{3}-\partial_{t}^{k}\bar{\partial}^{5-k}\eta^{3}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}&=\sum_{k=0}^{4}\|\partial_{t}^{k}\bar{\partial}^{5-k}\eta^{\lambda}(h^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha}\eta^{3}\partial_{\beta}\eta^{l}\delta_{\lambda l})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&=\sum_{k=0}^{4}\|\partial_{t}^{k}\bar{\partial}^{5-k}\eta^{\lambda}(h^{\alpha\beta}(\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{\alpha}v^{3})\partial_{\beta}\eta^{l}\delta_{\lambda l})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\sum_{k=0}^{4}\|\partial_{t}^{k}\eta\|_{H^{5-k}(\Gamma_{1})}\|h^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\beta}\eta^{l}\|_{H^{1.5}(\Gamma_{1})}T\|\bar{\partial}v\|_{H^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.56)

Now, we investigate the boundary estimate of
\eta
. The estimate of
I
,
III
,
IV
is identical to
[
10
]
. Our analysis aims to explicitly verify the dependence on
E^{\prime}_{\kappa}
. Readers may skip this section and proceed directly to
II
for the modified part.

Lemma 6.8

(The estimate for the fourth time-differentiated
\kappa
-problem)
.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{j}\phi\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{i}\phi+\int_{\Omega}J_{k}|\partial_{t}^{4}v|^{2}+\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}\Pi^{\mu}_{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\Pi_{\alpha}^{\mu}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\cdot n^{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E_{\kappa}^{\prime})+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi\|_{1}^{2}+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi\|^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(6.57)

Proof
.

Multiplying
J_{\kappa}
on (
3.15
), and taking
\partial_{t}^{4}
then testing with
\partial_{t}^{4}v
, we can write

\displaystyle\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}v^{i})\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{I}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}q)\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}-\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}-\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi^{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{II}.

(6.58)

We calculate the commutator term by term.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}v^{i})\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}&\sim\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}|\partial_{t}^{4}v|^{2}+\sum_{\textit{$i_{1}+i_{2}=4$, $i_{1}\geq 1$}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{i_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{i_{2}}\partial_{t}v^{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\\
&\sim\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{\Omega}J_{k}|\partial_{t}^{4}v|^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}J_{\kappa}|\partial_{t}^{4}v|^{2}+\sum_{\textit{$i_{1}+i_{2}=4$, $i_{1}\geq 1$}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{i_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{i_{2}}\partial_{t}v^{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\end{aligned}

(6.59)

From direct calculation

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \sum_{\textit{$i_{1}+i_{2}=4$, $i_{1}\geq 1$}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}|\partial_{t}^{i_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{i_{2}}\partial_{t}v^{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}|&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}J_{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}\\
&+\sum_{i=2}^{4}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{i}J_{\kappa}\|_{L^{3}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{5-i}v\|_{L^{6}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}J_{\kappa}\|_{2}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\sum_{i=2}^{4}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{i}J_{\kappa}\|_{0.5}\|\partial_{t}^{5-i}v\|_{1}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.60)

Therefore, we can write

\displaystyle I=\int_{\Omega}J_{k}|\partial_{t}^{4}v|^{2}+\mathcal{R},

(6.61)

where
|\mathcal{R}|\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})
. Using Piola’s formula, and integrating by part, we can show that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k}\partial_{k}q)\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}&=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{k}\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k}q)\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\\
&=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}q)\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i})}_{III}\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k}q)\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{k}v^{i}}_{IV}\end{aligned}

(6.62)

We can show that the estimate in
[
10
]
is still valid for our model. The third term in (
6.58
) reads

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}-\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}|&\lesssim\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=4}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}|\partial_{t}^{i_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{i_{2}}\phi^{\kappa}_{t}\partial_{t}^{i_{3}}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}|\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{4}J_{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\phi^{\kappa}_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\|J_{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi^{\kappa}_{t}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\|J_{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}\phi^{\kappa}_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\|\partial_{t}^{3}J_{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{i_{1}}\phi^{\kappa}_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\|\partial_{t}^{2}J_{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{i_{1}}\phi^{\kappa}_{t}\|_{L^{4}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{4}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=3}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\|\partial_{t}J_{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{i_{1}}\phi^{\kappa}_{t}\|_{L^{4}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{4}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\delta\int_{0}^{t}|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi^{\kappa}|^{2}+C(\delta)T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\end{aligned}

(6.63)

The fourth term in (
6.58
) leads to a cancellation, which we will see later. Now, we adapt the same strategy to deal with the second term in (
6.58
).

\displaystyle III=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{4}(-\sigma\sqrt{h}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n_{\kappa}n_{\kappa}^{i})\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{III_{1}}+\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(\kappa\Delta_{0}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})n_{\kappa}^{i})\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{III_{2}}.

(6.64)

We rewrite
III_{1}
as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} III_{1}&=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{4}(-\sigma\sqrt{h}\Delta_{h}(\eta^{i}))\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{III_{11}}+\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{4}(-\sigma\sqrt{h}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot(n_{\kappa}-n)n^{i})\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{III_{12}}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{4}(-\sigma\sqrt{h}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n_{\kappa}(n_{\kappa}^{i}-n^{i}))\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{III_{13}}\end{aligned}

(6.65)

Using (
2.35
) and integrating by part, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} III_{11}&=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}-\sigma\partial_{t}^{3}(\partial_{i}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}(\delta^{\alpha\lambda}-h^{lk}\partial_{l}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{k}\eta^{\alpha})\partial_{j}\partial_{t}\eta^{\lambda}+\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{t}\partial_{l}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha}))\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\alpha}\\
&=\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{3}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}(\delta^{\alpha\lambda}-h^{lk}\partial_{l}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{k}\eta^{\alpha})\partial_{j}\partial_{t}\eta^{\lambda}+\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{t}\partial_{l}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha})\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\alpha}\\
&=\underbrace{\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}(\delta^{\alpha\lambda}-h^{lk}\partial_{l}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{k}\eta^{\alpha})\partial_{j}\partial_{t}^{4}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\alpha}}_{III_{11,1}}\underbrace{+\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{l}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\alpha}}_{III_{11,2}}\\
&\underbrace{+3\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}(\delta^{\alpha\lambda}-h^{lk}\partial_{l}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{k}\eta^{\alpha}))\partial_{j}\partial_{t}^{3}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\alpha}+\partial_{t}(\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha})\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{l}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\alpha}}_{III_{11,3}}\\
&\underbrace{+3\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{2}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}(\delta^{\alpha\lambda}-h^{lk}\partial_{l}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{k}\eta^{\alpha}))\partial_{j}\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\alpha}+\partial_{t}^{2}(\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha})\partial_{t}^{2}\partial_{l}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\alpha}}_{III_{11,4}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{3}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}(\delta^{\alpha\lambda}-h^{lk}\partial_{l}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{k}\eta^{\alpha}))\partial_{j}\partial_{t}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\alpha}+\partial_{t}^{3}(\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha})\partial_{t}\partial_{l}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\alpha}}_{III_{11,5}}\end{aligned}

(6.66)

We estimate the lower order terms
III_{11,3}
,
III_{11,4}
,

as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} III_{11,3}+III_{11,4}+III_{11,5}&\sim\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=2}\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{i_{1}}\partial_{t}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}(\delta^{\alpha\lambda}-h^{lk}\partial_{l}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{k}\eta^{\alpha}))\partial_{t}^{i_{2}}\partial_{j}\partial_{t}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}|_{0}^{t}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=2}\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{i_{1}}(\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha})\partial_{t}^{i_{2}}\partial_{l}\partial_{t}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}|_{0}^{t}\\
&+\sigma\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=3}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{i_{1}}\partial_{t}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}(\delta^{\alpha\lambda}-h^{lk}\partial_{l}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{k}\eta^{\alpha}))\partial_{t}^{i_{2}}\partial_{j}\partial_{t}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\\
&+\sigma\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=3}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{i_{1}}\partial_{t}(\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha})\partial_{t}^{i_{2}}\partial_{l}\partial_{t}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\end{aligned}

(6.67)

Recall that
\sup_{t}(\sum_{k=0}^{4}\|\partial_{t}^{k}\bar{\partial}\eta\|_{H^{4-k}(\partial\Omega)}+\sum_{k=0}^{4}\|\partial_{t}^{k}h^{ij}\|_{H^{4-k}(\partial\Omega)})\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})
, and

\sup_{t}(\sum_{k=0}^{3}\|\partial_{t}^{k}\partial\eta\|_{H^{3-k}(\partial\Omega)}+\sum_{k=0}^{3}\|\partial_{t}^{k}h^{ij}\|_{H^{3-k}(\partial\Omega)})\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})
. Then we can show that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |III_{11,3}|+|III_{11,4}|+|III_{11,5}|&\lesssim\delta\|\bar{\partial}\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(6.68)

Now, we consider
III_{11,2}
. There exists an antisymmetric structure on

. (In

1) Rename

and

\displaystyle\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\alpha}=-\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\alpha}

(6.69)

2) Rename
\alpha
and

\displaystyle-\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\alpha}=-\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{k}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\partial_{j}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\lambda}

(6.70)

and

\displaystyle-\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{k}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\partial_{j}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\lambda}=\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\lambda}

(6.71)

Consequently,

\displaystyle\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\alpha}=\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\lambda}.

(6.72)

However, integrating by part allows us to write

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\lambda}&=\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}|_{0}^{t}\\
&-\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}(\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda})\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\partial_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}\\
&-\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\alpha}\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}\end{aligned}

(6.73)

Now, we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} III_{11,2}&=\frac{1}{2}\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}|_{0}^{t}\\

(6.74)

For the first term of , integrating by part on

and

(6.75)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}|_{0}^{t}&=-\frac{1}{2}\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{i}(\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\partial_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}|_{0}^{t}\\

Hence, we can conclude that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} III_{11,2}&=-\frac{1}{4}\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{i}(\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\partial_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}|_{0}^{t}\\

(6.77)

We can also have the following estimate

\displaystyle\|\bar{\partial}(\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{ik}h^{jl})\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.78)

Now, we use the interpolation argument again

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |III_{11,2}|&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}v\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+C(\delta)\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\\

&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}v\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{\frac{4}{3}}C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\\
&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})),\end{aligned}

(6.79)

after choosing an appropriate coefficient when we use Young’s inequality. The term
III_{11,1}
will provide us with the desired improved regularity on the boundary.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} III_{11,1}&=\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\Pi_{\alpha}^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\partial_{t}^{4}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\alpha}\\
&=\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}\partial_{t}^{4}\eta^{\lambda}\Pi^{\mu}_{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\alpha}\Pi_{\alpha}^{\mu}\\
&=\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{t}(\partial_{j}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}\Pi^{\mu}_{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\Pi_{\alpha}^{\mu})\\
&-\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\partial_{t}(\delta^{\alpha\lambda}-h^{kl}\partial_{k}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{l}\eta^{\beta})\\
&=\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}\Pi^{\mu}_{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\Pi_{\alpha}^{\mu}|_{0}^{t}\\
&-\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij})\partial_{j}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}\Pi^{\mu}_{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\Pi_{\alpha}^{\mu}\\
&-\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\partial_{t}(\delta^{\alpha\lambda}-h^{kl}\partial_{k}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{l}\eta^{\beta}).\end{aligned}

(6.80)

Since

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{t}(\delta^{\alpha\lambda}-h^{kl}\partial_{k}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{l}\eta^{\beta})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),

we can write that

\displaystyle|-\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij})\partial_{j}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}\Pi^{\mu}_{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\Pi_{\alpha}^{\mu}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})

(6.81)

\displaystyle|-\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\partial_{t}(\delta^{\alpha\lambda}-h^{kl}\partial_{k}\eta^{\alpha}\partial_{l}\eta^{\beta})|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.82)

Now, we have

\displaystyle III_{11,1}=\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}\Pi^{\mu}_{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\Pi_{\alpha}^{\mu}+\mathcal{R},

(6.83)

where
\mathcal{R}\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})
.
Therefore, combining (
6.68
), (
6.79
), and (

), we have

\displaystyle III_{11}=\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}\Pi^{\mu}_{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\Pi_{\alpha}^{\mu}+\mathcal{R},

(6.84)

where
\mathcal{R}\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1.5}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))
. Now we deal with
III_{12}
and
III_{13}
.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} III_{12}&\sim\underbrace{\sum_{k=1}^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{k}(-\sigma\sqrt{h}\Delta_{h}(\eta)n^{i})\partial_{t}^{4-k}(n_{\kappa}-n)\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{III_{12,1}}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{3}(-\sigma\sqrt{h}\Delta_{h}(\eta)n^{i})\partial_{t}(n_{\kappa}-n)\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{III_{12,2}}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}(-\sigma\sqrt{h}\Delta_{h}(\eta)n^{i})\partial_{t}^{4}(n_{\kappa}-n)\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{III_{12,3}}\end{aligned}

(6.85)

\displaystyle III_{13}=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{4}(-\sigma\sqrt{h}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n_{\kappa}(n_{\kappa}^{i}-n^{i}))\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}

(6.86)

Further,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |III_{12,1}|&\lesssim\sum_{k=1}^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{k}(-\sigma\sqrt{h}\Delta_{h}(\eta)n^{i})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{4-k}(n_{\kappa}-n)\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\sqrt{\kappa}T^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})(\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.87)

Similarly,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} III_{12,2}=\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{3}(h^{ij}\partial_{j}\eta n^{i})\partial_{i}\partial_{t}(n^{\kappa}-n)\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}+\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{3}(h^{ij}\partial_{j}\eta n^{i})\partial_{t}(n^{\kappa}-n)\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i},\end{aligned}

(6.88)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |III_{12,2}|&\lesssim\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{3}(h^{ij}\partial_{j}\eta n^{i})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{i}\partial_{t}(n^{\kappa}-n)\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{3}(h^{ij}\partial_{j}\eta n^{i})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}(n^{\kappa}-n)\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\sqrt{\kappa}\times\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\end{aligned}

(6.89)

For
III_{12,3}
, see our previous lemma and the discussion therein. It’s evident that
III_{13}
can be controlled in a similar fashion. We omit the details. Up to now, we have controlled

\displaystyle III_{1}=\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}\Pi^{\mu}_{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\Pi_{\alpha}^{\mu}+\mathcal{R},

(6.90)

where
|R|\lesssim C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))+\delta(\|v_{ttt}\|_{1.5}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2})
. We estimate the viscous term
III_{2}
.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} III_{2}&=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\Delta_{0}\partial_{t}^{4}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})n_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{III_{21}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{k=0}^{3}C_{4}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\Delta_{0}\partial_{t}^{k}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{4-k}n_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{III_{22}}.\end{aligned}

(6.91)

We estimate
III_{22}
first. When
k=0,1
, employing Hölder inequality (
L^{\infty}
-
L^{2}
-
L^{2}
)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |C_{4}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}-\kappa\Delta_{0}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{4}n_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}|&\leq|C_{4}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}-\kappa\partial_{t}^{k}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{4-k}n_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}|\\
&+|C_{4}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}-\kappa\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{k}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4-k}n_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}|\\
&+|C_{4}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}-\kappa\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{k}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{4-k}n_{\kappa}^{i}\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}|\\
&\lesssim|C_{4}^{k}|\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\partial_{t}^{k}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\|_{H^{2.5}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{4-k}n_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}.\end{aligned}

(6.92)

When
k=2
, we use Hölder inequality (
L^{4}
-
L^{4}
-
L^{2}
)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |C_{4}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\Delta_{0}\partial_{t}^{k}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{4-k}n_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}|&\leq|C_{4}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\partial_{t}^{k}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{4-k}n_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}|\\
&+|C_{4}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{k}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4-k}n_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}|\\
&+|C_{4}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{k}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{4-k}n_{\kappa}^{i}\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}|\\
&\lesssim|C_{4}^{k}|\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\partial_{t}^{k}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\|_{H^{1.5}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{4-k}n_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{H^{1.5}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}.\end{aligned}

(6.93)

When
k=3
, applying Hölder inequality (
L^{2}
-
L^{\infty}
-
L^{2}
)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\Delta_{0}\partial_{t}^{3}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\partial_{t}n_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}|&\leq|4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\partial_{t}^{3}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\partial_{t}n_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}|\\
&+|4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}n_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}|\\
&+|4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\partial_{t}n_{\kappa}^{i}\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}|\\
&\lesssim|\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\partial_{t}^{3}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}n_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}.\end{aligned}

(6.94)

Consequently, we have

\displaystyle|III_{22}|\lesssim C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}.

(6.95)

The term
III_{21}
will contribute another boundary regular term we need.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} III_{21}&=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\partial_{t}^{4}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})n_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\partial_{t}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{l}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\bar{\partial}_{l}(\partial_{t}^{4}v\cdot n_{\kappa})\\
&=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}-\kappa\partial_{t}^{4}v\cdot n_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\cdot n_{\kappa}-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}_{l}(\partial_{t}^{4}v\cdot n_{\kappa})\bar{\partial}_{l}(\partial_{t}^{4}v\cdot n_{\kappa})\\
&+\sum_{k=0}^{3}C_{4}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\partial_{t}^{k}v^{j}\partial_{t}^{4-k}n_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v\cdot n_{\kappa}+\sum_{k=0}^{3}C_{4}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}_{l}(\partial_{t}^{k}v^{j}\partial_{t}^{4-k}n_{\kappa})\bar{\partial}_{l}(\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}n_{\kappa}^{i})\\
&=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\partial_{t}^{4}v\cdot n_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\cdot n_{\kappa}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}_{l}(\partial_{t}^{4}v\cdot n_{\kappa})\bar{\partial}_{l}(\partial_{t}^{4}v\cdot n_{\kappa})}_{III_{21,1}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{k=0}^{3}C_{4}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\partial_{t}^{k}v^{j}\partial_{t}^{4-k}n_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v\cdot n_{\kappa}}_{III_{21,2}}\underbrace{+\sum_{k=0}^{3}C_{4}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{k}v^{j}\partial_{t}^{4-k}n_{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}n_{\kappa}^{i}}_{III_{21,3}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{k=0}^{3}C_{4}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{k}v^{j}\partial_{t}^{4-k}n_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\bar{\partial}_{l}n_{\kappa}^{i}}_{III_{21,4}}\underbrace{+\sum_{k=0}^{3}C_{4}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\partial_{t}^{k}v^{j}\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4-k}n_{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}n_{\kappa}^{i}}_{III_{21,5}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{k=0}^{3}C_{4}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\partial_{t}^{k}v^{j}\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4-k}n_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\bar{\partial}_{l}n_{\kappa}^{i}}_{III21,6}.\end{aligned}

(6.96)

From direct computation, one can see that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |III_{21,2}|&\lesssim\sum_{k=0}^{3}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\partial_{t}^{k}v^{j}\partial_{t}^{3-k}\partial_{t}n_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|n_{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\kappa\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(6.97)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |III_{21,3}|&\lesssim\sum_{k=0}^{3}|C_{4}^{k}|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\|\partial_{t}^{k}\bar{\partial}_{l}v^{j}\partial_{t}^{3-k}\partial_{t}n_{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|n_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\kappa\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(6.98)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |III_{21,4}|&\lesssim\sum_{k=0}^{3}|C_{4}^{k}|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\|\partial_{t}^{k}\bar{\partial}_{l}v^{j}\partial_{t}^{4-k}n_{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}_{l}n_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\kappa\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.99)

Now, invoking (
6.48
), we have

\displaystyle|III_{21,5}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|v^{j}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{l}n_{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|n_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}

(6.100)

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{2}|C_{4}^{k+1}|\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{t}v^{j}\partial_{t}^{3-k}\bar{\partial}_{l}n_{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|n_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}

(6.101)

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}(\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{2}(\Gamma_{1})})\kappa\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}\times(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))

(6.102)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+\int_{0}^{t}(\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}

(6.103)

\displaystyle\lesssim C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+(\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2})^{2}+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2},

(6.104)

III_{21,6}
can be estimated in a similar way,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |III_{21,6}|&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|v^{j}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{l}n_{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}_{l}n_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\sum_{k=0}^{2}|C_{4}^{k+1}|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\|\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{t}v^{j}\partial_{t}^{3-k}\bar{\partial}_{l}n_{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}_{l}n_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+(\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2})^{2}+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}.\end{aligned}

(6.105)

Consequently, we have

\displaystyle III_{21}=\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\cdot n^{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}+\mathcal{R},

(6.106)

where
\mathcal{R}\lesssim C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+(\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2})^{2}+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}
.
Thus, combining (
6.95
) and (
6.106
),
we have

\displaystyle III_{2}=\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\cdot n^{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}+\mathcal{R},

(6.107)

where
\mathcal{R}\lesssim C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+(\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2})^{2}+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}
.
Combining (
6.90
) and (
6.107
), one can derive that

\displaystyle III=\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}\Pi^{\mu}_{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\Pi_{\alpha}^{\mu}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\cdot n^{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+\mathcal{R},

(6.108)

where
\mathcal{R}\lesssim C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+(\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2})^{2}+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}
.

Now, we estimate the interior term
IV
.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV=\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}q(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k}\partial_{k}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{IV_{1}}\underbrace{-\sum_{l=1}^{4}C_{4}^{l}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{l}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k})\partial_{t}^{4-l}q\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{k}v^{i}}_{IV_{{2}}}\end{aligned}

(6.109)

We estimate
IV_{2}
first.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{2}=\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k})q\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{k}v^{i}}_{IV_{21}}\underbrace{-\sum_{l=1}^{3}C_{4}^{l}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{l}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k})\partial_{t}^{4-l}q\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{k}v^{i}}_{IV_{22}}\end{aligned}

(6.110)

Integrating by part in
IV_{22}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{22}&=-\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=1}^{3}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k}\partial_{t}^{4-l_{1}-l_{2}}q\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{k}v^{i}\\
&=\underbrace{-\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=1}^{3}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\partial_{t}^{4-l_{1}-l_{2}}q\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{IV_{22,1}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{l_{1}+l_{2}=1}^{3}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{k}(\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k})\partial_{t}^{4-l_{1}-l_{2}}q\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{IV_{22,2}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=1}^{3}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k}\partial_{k}\partial_{t}^{4-l_{1}-l_{2}}q\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{IV_{22.3}}.\end{aligned}

(6.111)

Recall that
\sup_{t}\|q\|_{4.5}+\|q_{t}\|_{3.5}+\|q_{tt}\|_{2.5}\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})
,
\|q_{ttt}\|_{1}\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})
. We can write

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{22,2}|&\lesssim\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=1}^{1}|C_{4}^{l}||C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}|\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{k}(\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=2}^{3}|C_{4}^{l}||C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}|\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{k}(\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k})\|_{L^{3}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4-l_{1}-l_{2}}q\|_{L^{6}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(6.112)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{22,3}|&\lesssim\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=1}^{1}|C_{4}^{l}||C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}|\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=2}^{3}|C_{4}^{l}||C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}|\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k}\|_{L^{3}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{k}\partial_{t}^{4-l_{1}-l_{2}}q\|_{L^{6}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\end{aligned}

(6.113)

Plunging in the Dirichlet boundary condition of q, we can express
IV_{22,1}
as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{22,1}&=\underbrace{4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\partial_{t}^{3}(\sigma(\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n_{\kappa}))\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{IV_{22,11}}\\
&\underbrace{-4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\partial_{t}^{3}(\kappa\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}((1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n^{\kappa})))\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{IV_{22,12}}\\
&\underbrace{-\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=2}^{3}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\partial_{t}^{4-l_{1}-l_{2}}q\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{IV_{22,13}},\end{aligned}

(6.114)

Integrating by part in time, we can write
II_{22,11}
as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{22,11}&=\underbrace{-4\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\partial_{t}^{3}(-\sigma(\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n_{\kappa}))\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}|_{0}^{t}}_{IV_{22,111}}\\
&\underbrace{-4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\partial_{t}^{3}(-\sigma(\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n_{\kappa}))\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}}_{IV_{22,112}}\end{aligned}

(6.115)

We expand
IV_{22,111}
as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{22,111}&=\underbrace{4\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}n_{\kappa}^{j}\partial_{t}^{3}\Delta_{h}(\eta^{j})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}|_{0}^{t}}_{IV_{22,111,1}}\\
&\underbrace{+4\sigma C_{3}^{k}\sum_{k=1}^{3}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\partial_{t}^{k}(\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}n_{\kappa}^{j})\partial_{t}^{3-k}\Delta_{h}(\eta^{j})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}|_{0}^{t}}_{IV_{22,111,2}}\end{aligned}

(6.116)

Integrating by part, one can see that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{22,111,1}&=-4\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{\nu}(\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}n_{\kappa}^{j})\partial_{t}^{3}(h_{\kappa}^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\mu}\eta^{j})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}|_{0}^{t}\\
&-4\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}n_{\kappa}^{j}\partial_{t}^{3}(h_{\kappa}^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\mu}\eta^{j})\partial_{\nu}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}|_{0}^{t},\end{aligned}

(6.117)

Therefore.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{22,111,1}|&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+\sigma\|\partial_{\nu}(\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}n_{\kappa}^{j})\|_{L^{4}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}(h_{\kappa}^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\mu}\eta^{j})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\|_{L^{4}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\sigma\|\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}n_{\kappa}^{j}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}(h_{\kappa}^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\mu}\eta^{j})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{\nu}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(6.118)

From a direct calculation,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{22,111,2}|&\lesssim 4\sigma C_{3}^{k+1}\sum_{k=0}^{2}\|\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{t}(\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}n_{\kappa}^{j})\partial_{t}^{2-k}\Delta_{h}(\eta^{j})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(6.119)

Combining (
6.118
) and (
6.119
), one can arrive at

\displaystyle|IV_{22,111}|\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(6.120)

For
IV_{22,112}
, a direct calculation shows that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{22,112}&=\underbrace{4\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{2}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\partial_{t}^{3}(\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n_{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}}_{IV_{22,112,1}}\\
&\underbrace{+4\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\partial_{t}^{4}(\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n_{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}}_{IV_{22,112,2}}\end{aligned}

(6.121)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{22,112,1}|&\lesssim\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{2}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\sigma(\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n_{\kappa}))\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.122)

Expanding
IV_{22,112,2}
, and integrating by part, one can write as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{22,112,2}&=4\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}n_{\kappa}^{j}\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{\mu}(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\nu}\eta^{j})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\\
&+4\sigma\sum_{k=1}^{4}C_{4}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\partial_{t}^{k}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}n_{\kappa}^{j})\partial_{t}^{4-k}\partial_{\mu}(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\nu}\eta^{j})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\\
&=-4\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{\mu}(\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}n_{\kappa}^{j})\partial_{t}^{4}(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\nu}\eta^{j})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\\
&-4\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}n_{\kappa}^{j}\partial_{t}^{4}(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\nu}\eta^{j})\partial_{\mu}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\\
&+4\sigma\sum_{k=1}^{4}C_{4}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\partial_{t}^{k}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}n_{\kappa}^{j})\partial_{t}^{4-k}\partial_{\mu}(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\nu}\eta^{j})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}.\end{aligned}

(6.123)

Hence,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{22,112,2}|&\lesssim\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{\mu}(\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}n_{\kappa}^{j})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{4}(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\nu}\eta^{j})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}n_{\kappa}^{j}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{4}(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\nu}\eta^{j})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{\mu}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\sigma\sum_{k=0}^{3}C_{4}^{k+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{t}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}n_{\kappa}^{j})\partial_{t}^{3-k}\partial_{\mu}(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\nu}\eta^{j})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.124)

Hence, combining (
6.122
) and (
6.124
), one can deduce that

\displaystyle|IV_{22,112}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.125)

Combining (
6.120
) and (
6.125
), we have

\displaystyle|IV_{22,11}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(6.126)

Now, considering
IV_{22,12}
,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{22,12}&=\underbrace{-4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\partial_{t}^{3}((1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n^{\kappa}))\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{IV_{22,121}}\\
&\underbrace{-4\sum_{k=1}^{3}C_{3}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\partial_{t}^{k}\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\partial_{t}^{3-k}(1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n^{\kappa}))\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{IV_{22,122}}\end{aligned}

(6.127)

Integrating by part for
IV_{22,121}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{22,121}&=-4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\partial_{t}^{3}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\\
&-4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}_{l}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\\
&-4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}_{l}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}.\end{aligned}

(6.128)

Thus,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{22,121}|&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{\kappa}\|\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{\kappa}\|\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}_{l}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{\kappa}\|\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}_{l}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{\kappa}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.129)

The control of
IV_{22,122}
is straight,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{22,122}|&\lesssim\sum_{k=0}^{2}C_{3}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{\kappa}\|\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{t}\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\partial_{t}^{2-k}(1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n^{\kappa}))\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{\kappa}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.130)

Combining (
6.129
) and (
6.130
) gives us

\displaystyle|IV_{22,12}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.131)

We estimate of
IV_{22,13}
via integrating by part in time,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{22,13}&=-\underbrace{\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=2}^{3}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\partial_{t}^{4-l_{1}-l_{2}}q\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}|_{0}^{t}}_{IV_{22,131}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=2}^{3}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\partial_{t}^{4-l_{1}-l_{2}}q\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}}_{IV_{22,132}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=2}^{3}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\partial_{t}^{5-l_{1}-l_{2}}q\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}}_{IV_{22,133}}\end{aligned}

(6.132)

Similarly,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{22,131}|&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=2}^{2}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\|\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\|_{L^{4}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{2}(\sigma(\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n_{\kappa}))\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\|_{L^{4}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=2}^{2}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\|\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\|_{L^{4}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{2}(\kappa\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}((1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n^{\kappa}))\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\|_{L^{4}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=3}^{3}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\|\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}q\|_{H^{1.5}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})),\end{aligned}

(6.133)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{22,132}|&\lesssim\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=2}^{3}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{4-l_{1}-l_{2}}q\|_{L^{4}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\|_{L^{4}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(6.134)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{22,133}&=\underbrace{+\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=3}^{3}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\partial_{t}^{2}(\sigma\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n_{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}}_{IV_{22,133,1}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=3}^{3}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\partial_{t}^{5-l_{1}-l_{2}}(\kappa\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}(1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n^{\kappa}))\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}}_{IV_{22,133,2}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=2}^{2}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\partial_{t}^{3}(\sigma\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n_{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}}_{IV_{22,133,3}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=2}^{2}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\partial_{t}^{3}(\kappa\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}(1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n^{\kappa}))\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}}_{IV_{22,133,4}}.\end{aligned}

(6.135)

The first three terms in (
6.135
) can be estimated directly, which reads

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{22,133,1}|&\lesssim\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=3}^{3}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\|_{L^{4}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{2}(\sigma\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n_{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\|_{L^{4}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(6.136)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{22,133,2}|&\lesssim\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=3}^{3}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\|_{L^{4}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{2}(\kappa\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}(1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n^{\kappa}))\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(6.137)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{22,133,3}|&\lesssim\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=2}^{2}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\|_{L^{4}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}(\sigma\frac{\sqrt{h}}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n_{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\|_{L^{4}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.138)

For
IV_{22,133,4}
, we integrate it by part,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{22,133,4}&=\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=2}^{2}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}})\partial_{t}^{3}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\\
&+\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=2}^{2}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}_{\mu}(\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}))\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\\
&+\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=2}^{2}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}})\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}v^{i}\\
&+\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=2}^{2}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\sum_{k=1}^{3}C_{3}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\partial_{t}^{k}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}})\partial_{t}^{3-k}(1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\end{aligned}

(6.139)

Therefore,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{22,133,4}|&\lesssim\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=2}^{2}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=2}^{2}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\bar{\partial}_{\mu}(\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}}))\|_{L^{4}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\|_{L^{4}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=2}^{2}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\sum_{l=l_{1}+l_{2}=2}^{2}C_{4}^{l}C_{l_{1}+l_{2}}^{l_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{2}C_{3}^{k+1}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{t}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}})\partial_{t}^{2-k}(1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.140)

Combining (
6.136
), (
6.137
), (
6.138
), and(
6.140
), we obtain

\displaystyle|IV_{22,133}|

\displaystyle\lesssim T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.141)

Combining (
6.133
), (
6.134
), and (
6.141
), we have

\displaystyle|IV_{22,13}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(6.142)

Combining (
6.126
), (
6.131
), and(
6.142
), we have

\displaystyle|IV_{22,1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(6.143)

Combining (

), we have

\displaystyle|IV_{22}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(6.144)

The estimate for
IV_{21}
is similar to
[
15
]
. The readers may also refer to
[
22
]
for a similar case.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{21}&=\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(JA_{i}^{k})q\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{k}v^{i}}_{IV_{21,1}}\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k}-JA_{i}^{k})q\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{k}v^{i}}_{IV_{21,2}},\end{aligned}

(6.145)

where
J=\det\nabla\eta
. Thus, we can say
JA_{i}^{k}=Cof(\nabla\eta)
and
J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k}=Cof(\nabla\eta^{\kappa})
. Let
H=Cof
as the function. Similarly as the way we deal with
n-n^{\kappa}
, one can show that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} H(\nabla\eta^{\kappa})-H(\nabla\eta)&=\int_{0}^{1}DH(\nabla\eta+s(\nabla\eta^{\kappa}-\nabla\eta))ds\times(\nabla\eta^{\kappa}-\nabla\eta).\end{aligned}

(6.146)

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \partial_{t}^{4}(H(\nabla\eta^{\kappa})-H(\nabla\eta))&=\int_{0}^{1}DH(\nabla\eta+s(\nabla\eta^{\kappa}-\nabla\eta))ds\times\partial_{t}^{4}(\nabla\eta^{\kappa}-\nabla\eta)\\
&+\sum_{k=1}^{4}C_{4}^{k}\partial_{t}^{k}\int_{0}^{1}DH(\nabla\eta+s(\nabla\eta^{\kappa}-\nabla\eta))ds\times\partial_{t}^{4-k}(\nabla\eta^{\kappa}-\nabla\eta),\end{aligned}

(6.147)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\partial_{t}^{4}(H(\nabla\eta^{\kappa})-H(\nabla\eta))\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}&\lesssim\|\int_{0}^{1}DH(\nabla\eta+s(\nabla\eta^{\kappa}-\nabla\eta))ds\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\times\|\partial_{t}^{4}(\nabla\eta^{\kappa}-\nabla\eta)\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+4\|\partial_{t}\int_{0}^{1}DH(\nabla\eta+s(\nabla\eta^{\kappa}-\nabla\eta))ds\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\times\|\partial_{t}^{3}(\nabla\eta^{\kappa}-\nabla\eta)\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{k=2}^{3}C_{4}^{k}\|\partial_{t}^{k}\int_{0}^{1}DH(\nabla\eta+s(\nabla\eta^{\kappa}-\nabla\eta))ds\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\times\|\partial_{t}^{4-k}(\nabla\eta^{\kappa}-\nabla\eta)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}^{4}\int_{0}^{1}DH(\nabla\eta+s(\nabla\eta^{\kappa}-\nabla\eta))ds\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\times\|(\nabla\eta^{\kappa}-\nabla\eta)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\|\int_{0}^{1}DH(\nabla\eta+s(\nabla\eta^{\kappa}-\nabla\eta))ds\|_{H^{2}(\Omega)}\times\kappa\|v_{ttt}\|_{H^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+4\|\partial_{t}\int_{0}^{1}DH(\nabla\eta+s(\nabla\eta^{\kappa}-\nabla\eta))ds\|_{H^{2}(\Omega)}\times\kappa\|v_{tt}\|_{H^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{k=0}^{1}C_{4}^{k+2}\|\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{t}^{2}\int_{0}^{1}DH(\nabla\eta+s(\nabla\eta^{\kappa}-\nabla\eta))ds\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\times\kappa\|\partial_{t}^{1-k}v\|_{H^{3.5}(\Omega)}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}^{4}\int_{0}^{1}DH(\nabla\eta+s(\nabla\eta^{\kappa}-\nabla\eta))ds\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\times\kappa\|\eta\|_{H^{3.5}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\kappa\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|v_{ttt}\|_{H^{2}(\Omega)}+\kappa\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.148)

Hence,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{21,2}|&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k}-JA_{i}^{k})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|q\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{k}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}(\kappa\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|v_{ttt}\|_{H^{2}(\Omega)}+\kappa\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(6.149)

We expand
IV_{21,1}
first

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{21,1}&=\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}JA_{i}^{k}q\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{k}v^{i}}_{IV_{22,11}}\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J\partial_{t}^{4}A_{i}^{k}q\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{k}v^{i}}_{IV_{21,12}}\\
&\underbrace{-\sum_{k=1}^{3}C_{4}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{k}J\partial_{t}^{4-k}A_{i}^{k}q\partial_{k}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{IV_{22,13}}.\end{aligned}

(6.150)

We deal with
IV_{21,13}
first.

\displaystyle\sup_{t}\|q\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\lesssim\sup_{t}\|q\|_{H^{2}(\Omega)}\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{0}^{t}\|q_{t}\|_{H^{2}(\Omega)}\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.151)

One can compute that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{21,13}=-\sum_{l=0}^{2}C_{4}^{l+1}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{l}\partial_{t}J\partial_{t}^{2-l}\partial_{t}A_{i}^{k}q\partial_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}|_{0}^{t}+\sum_{l=0}^{2}C_{4}^{l+1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}^{l}\partial_{t}J\partial_{t}^{2-l}\partial_{t}A_{i}^{k}q)\partial_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i},\end{aligned}

(6.152)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{21,13}|&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+\sum_{l=0}^{2}C_{4}^{l+1}\|\partial_{t}^{l}\partial_{t}J\partial_{t}^{2-l}\partial_{t}A_{i}^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|q\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{l=0}^{2}C_{4}^{l+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}^{l}\partial_{t}J\partial_{t}^{2-l}\partial_{t}A_{i}^{k})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|q\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{l=0}^{2}C_{4}^{l+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{l}\partial_{t}J\partial_{t}^{2-l}\partial_{t}A_{i}^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}q\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{k}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(6.153)

Similarly,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{21,12}&=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J\partial_{t}^{3}(-A_{i}^{l}\partial_{l}v^{j}A_{j}^{k})q\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{k}v^{i}\\
&=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{l}v^{j}A_{i}^{l}A_{j}^{k}q\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{k}v^{i}}_{IV_{22,121}}\underbrace{+\sum_{k=1}^{3}C_{3}^{k}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}Jq\partial_{t}^{k}(A_{i}^{l}A_{j}^{k})\partial_{t}^{3-k}\partial_{l}v^{j}\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{k}v^{i}}_{IV_{22,122}}\end{aligned}

(6.154)

Applying a similar method, one can find that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{21,122}&=\sum_{k=0}^{2}C_{3}^{k+1}\int_{\Omega}Jq\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{t}(A_{i}^{l}A_{j}^{k})\partial_{t}^{2-k}\partial_{l}v^{j}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\\
&-\sum_{k=0}^{2}C_{3}^{k+1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}qJ\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{t}(A_{i}^{l}A_{j}^{k})\partial_{t}^{2-k}\partial_{l}v^{j}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\\
&-\sum_{k=0}^{2}C_{3}^{k+1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}q\partial_{t}J\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{t}(A_{i}^{l}A_{j}^{k})\partial_{t}^{2-k}\partial_{l}v^{j}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\\
&-\sum_{k=0}^{2}C_{3}^{k+1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}qJ\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{t}(A_{i}^{l}A_{j}^{k})\partial_{t}^{2-k}\partial_{l}v^{j})\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i},\end{aligned}

(6.155)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{21,122}|&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+\sum_{k=0}^{2}C_{3}^{k+1}\|J\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|q\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{t}(A_{i}^{l}A_{j}^{k})\partial_{t}^{2-k}\partial_{l}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{k=0}^{2}C_{3}^{k+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}q\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|J\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{t}(A_{i}^{l}A_{j}^{k})\partial_{t}^{2-k}\partial_{l}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{k=0}^{2}C_{3}^{k+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|q\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}J\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{t}(A_{i}^{l}A_{j}^{k})\partial_{t}^{2-k}\partial_{l}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{k=0}^{2}C_{3}^{k+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|q\|_{L^{\infty}}\|J\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{t}(A_{i}^{l}A_{j}^{k})\partial_{t}^{2-k}\partial_{l}v^{j})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(6.156)

Also, we can write

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{21,121}&=\int_{\Omega}JA_{i}^{l}A_{j}^{k}q\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{l}v^{j}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}qJA_{i}^{l}A_{j}^{k}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{l}v^{j}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\\
&-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}q\partial_{t}(JA_{i}^{l}A_{j}^{k})\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{l}v^{j}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}qJA_{i}^{l}A_{j}^{k}\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{l}v^{j}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\end{aligned}

(6.157)

Notice that the last term in (
6.157
) coincides with
-IV_{21,121}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{21,121}&=\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}JA_{i}^{l}A_{j}^{k}q\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{l}v^{j}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}qJA_{i}^{l}A_{j}^{k}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{l}v^{j}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\\
&-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}q\partial_{t}(JA_{i}^{l}A_{j}^{k})\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{l}v^{j}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\end{aligned}

(6.158)

With the help of Sobolev interpolation theorem, it follows that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{21,121}|&\lesssim\|JA_{i}^{l}A_{j}^{k}q\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{l}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}q\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|JA_{i}^{l}A_{j}^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{l}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\|q\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}(JA_{i}^{l}A_{j}^{k})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{l}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}^{2}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\\
&\lesssim(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{\frac{4}{3}}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\\
&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(6.159)

Combining (
6.156
) and (
6.159
), we have

\displaystyle|IV_{21,12}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(6.160)

We employ the Jacobi’s formula to expand
IV_{21,11}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{21,11}&=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{3}(JA_{l}^{j}\partial_{j}v^{l})A_{i}^{k}q\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{k}v^{i}\\
&=\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}qJA_{l}^{j}A_{i}^{k}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{j}v^{l}\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{k}v^{i}}_{IV_{21,111}}\underbrace{-\sum_{s=1}^{3}C_{3}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}A_{i}^{k}q\partial_{t}^{s}(JA_{l}^{j})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{j}v^{l}\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{k}v^{i}}_{IV_{21,112}}\end{aligned}

(6.161)

A similar method implies that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{21,112}&=-\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\int_{\Omega}A_{i}^{k}q\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}(JA_{l}^{j})\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{j}v^{l}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}|_{0}^{t}\\
&-\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}qA_{i}^{k}\partial_{t}^{s}(JA_{l}^{j})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{j}v^{l}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\\
&-\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}q\partial_{t}A_{i}^{k}\partial_{t}^{s}(JA_{l}^{j})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{j}v^{l}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\\
&-\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}qA_{i}^{k}\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}^{s}(JA_{l}^{j})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{j}v^{l})\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i},\end{aligned}

(6.162)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{21,112}|&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\|A_{i}^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|q\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}(JA_{l}^{j})\partial_{t}^{2-s}\partial_{j}v^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}q\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|A_{i}^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{s}(JA_{l}^{j})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{j}v^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|q\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}A_{i}^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{s}(JA_{l}^{j})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{j}v^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{3}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|q\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|A_{i}^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}^{s}(JA_{l}^{j})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{j}v^{l})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(6.163)

In a similar reason,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{21,111}&=-\int_{\Omega}qJA_{l}^{j}A_{i}^{k}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{j}v^{l}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}|_{0}^{t}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}qJA_{l}^{j}A_{i}^{k}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{j}v^{l}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}q\partial_{t}(JA_{l}^{j}A_{i}^{k})\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{j}v^{l}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}qJA_{l}^{j}A_{i}^{k}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{j}v^{l}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}.\end{aligned}

(6.164)

The last term aligns with
-IV_{21,111}
,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{21,111}&=-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}qJA_{l}^{j}A_{i}^{k}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{j}v^{l}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}|_{0}^{t}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}qJA_{l}^{j}A_{i}^{k}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{j}v^{l}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}q\partial_{t}(JA_{l}^{j}A_{i}^{k})\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{j}v^{l}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}.\end{aligned}

(6.165)

Thus,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{21,111}|&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+\|q\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|JA_{l}^{j}A_{i}^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{j}v^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}q\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|JA_{l}^{j}A_{i}^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{j}v^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\|q\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}(JA_{l}^{j}A_{i}^{k})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{j}v^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(6.166)

Combining (
6.163
) and (
6.166
), we have

\displaystyle|IV_{21,11}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(6.167)

Combining (
6.153
), (
6.160
) and (
6.167
), we have

\displaystyle|IV_{21,1}|\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(6.168)

Combining (
6.149
), (
6.168
), we have

\displaystyle|IV_{21}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(6.169)

Combining (
6.144
) and (
6.169
), we have

\displaystyle|IV_{2}|\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(6.170)

Now, we estimate
IV_{1}
, using the incompressible condition,

\displaystyle IV_{1}=\sum_{l=1}^{4}C_{4}^{l}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}q\partial_{t}^{l}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k}\partial_{t}^{4-l}\partial_{k}v^{i}.

(6.171)

Thus, we can write
IV_{1}
as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{1}&=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}q\partial_{t}^{4}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k}\partial_{k}v^{i}}_{IV_{11}}\underbrace{+4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}q\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{k}v^{i}}_{IV_{12}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{l=2}^{3}C_{4}^{l}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}q\partial_{t}^{l}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k}\partial_{t}^{4-l}\partial_{k}v^{i}}_{IV_{13}}\end{aligned}

(6.172)

We estimate
IV_{13}
first. Integrating by part in time,

\displaystyle IV_{13}=+\sum_{l=2}^{3}C_{4}^{l}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{3}q\partial_{t}^{l}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k}\partial_{t}^{4-l}\partial_{k}v^{i}|_{0}^{t}-\sum_{l=0}^{1}C_{4}^{l+2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{3}q\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l}\partial_{t}^{2}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k}\partial_{t}^{1-l}\partial_{t}\partial_{k}v^{i}).

(6.173)

We can show that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{13}|&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+\sum_{l=2}^{3}C_{4}^{l}\|J_{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{l}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k}\partial_{t}^{4-l}\partial_{k}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{l=0}^{1}C_{4}^{l+2}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{l}\partial_{t}^{2}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{k}\partial_{t}^{1-l}\partial_{t}\partial_{k}v^{i})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(6.174)

We deal with the lower order part in
IV_{11}
in a similar way:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{11}&=\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}q\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{l}v_{\kappa}^{j}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v^{i}}_{IV_{11,1}}\underbrace{-\sum_{s=1}^{3}C_{3}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}q\partial_{t}^{s}((A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{l}v_{\kappa}^{j}\partial_{k}v^{i}}_{IV_{11,2}}\end{aligned}

(6.175)

Integrating by part in time,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{11,2}&=\sum_{s=1}^{3}C_{3}^{s}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{3}q\partial_{t}^{s}((A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{l}v_{\kappa}^{j}\partial_{k}v^{i}|_{0}^{t}\\
&-\sum_{s=1}^{3}C_{3}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{3}q\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}^{s}((A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{l}v_{\kappa}^{j}\partial_{k}v^{i}J_{\kappa})\end{aligned}

(6.176)

using a similar estimate, it follows that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{11,2}|&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+\sum_{s=1}^{3}C_{3}^{s}\|J_{\kappa}\partial_{k}v^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{s}((A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{l}v_{\kappa}^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{s=1}^{3}C_{3}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}^{s}((A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{l}v_{\kappa}^{j})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{k}v^{i}J_{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\\
