&+\sum_{s=1}^{3}C_{3}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{s}((A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{l}v_{\kappa}^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}(\partial_{k}v^{i}J_{\kappa})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(6.177)

Combining (
6.172
), (
6.174
), (
6.175
), and (
6.177
), we can summarize that

\displaystyle IV_{1}=\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}q\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{l}v_{\kappa}^{j}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v^{i}}_{IV_{11,1}}\underbrace{-4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}q(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{l}v^{j}}_{IV_{12}}+\mathcal{R},

(6.178)

where
\mathcal{R}\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))
. We deal with these two terms in a similar way. We only show the
IV_{12}
as an example

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{12}=\underbrace{-4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l}q)(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{l}v^{j}}_{IV_{12,1}}\underbrace{+4\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{s}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l})\partial_{t}^{4-s}q(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{l}v^{j}}_{IV_{12,2}}.\end{aligned}

(6.179)

Clearly, we can estimate
IV_{12,2}
as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{12,2}|&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l})(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{l}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+4\sum_{s=2}^{4}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{s}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4-s}q(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{l}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.180)

For
IV_{12,1}
, integrating by part

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{12,1}&=\underbrace{4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{4}(\sigma(\sqrt{h}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n))(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}}_{IV_{12,11}}\\
&\underbrace{-4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{4}(\kappa(1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n_{\kappa}))(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}}_{IV_{12,12}}\\
&\underbrace{+4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l}\partial_{l}q)(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}}_{IV_{12,13}}\\
&\underbrace{+4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l}q)\partial_{l}((A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}}_{IV_{12,14}}\end{aligned}

(6.181)

We control
IV_{12,11}
by

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{12,11}&=-4\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{4}(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\nu}\eta)\cdot n(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\\
&-4\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{4}(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\nu}\eta)\cdot\partial_{\mu}(n(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\\
&+4\sigma\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{\mu}(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\nu}\eta)\cdot\partial_{t}^{4-s}n(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j},\end{aligned}

(6.182)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{12,11}|&\lesssim 4\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{4}(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\nu}\eta)\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|n(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{\mu}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1}}\\
&+4\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{4}(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\nu}\eta)\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\cdot\|\partial_{\mu}(n(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+4\sigma\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{\mu}(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\nu}\eta)\cdot\partial_{t}^{4-s}n\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\end{aligned}

(6.183)

Similarly,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{12,12}&=-4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\partial_{t}^{4}(\kappa(v\cdot n_{\kappa}))(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\\
&-4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}(\kappa\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}(v\cdot n_{\kappa}))\bar{\partial}_{l}((A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\\
&-4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}(\kappa\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}(v\cdot n_{\kappa}))(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j},\end{aligned}

(6.184)

Since

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}&\lesssim\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}\|n_{\kappa}\|_{H^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}+\|v\|_{H^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}n_{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}+\sum_{k=0}^{2}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{t}v\partial_{t}^{2-k}\partial_{t}n_{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim(\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}+\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{2}(\Omega)})(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))+\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(6.185)

we can calculate that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{12,12}|&\lesssim 4\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{\kappa}\|\partial_{t}^{4}(\sqrt{\kappa}(v\cdot n_{\kappa}))\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+4\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{\kappa}(\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}(v\cdot n_{\kappa}))\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}_{l}((A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+4\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{\kappa}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1}}\|(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}_{l}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.186)

Now, plugging Euler equation into
IV_{12,13}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{12,13}&=\underbrace{-4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}v_{i})(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}}_{IV_{12,131}}\underbrace{-4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}}_{IV_{12,132}}\\
&\underbrace{-4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi^{\kappa})(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}}_{IV_{12,133}}\end{aligned}

(6.187)

Using our estimate for
\phi
,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{12,133}&=-4\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{3}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi^{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}|_{0}^{t}\\
&+4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{3}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi^{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\\
&+4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{3}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi^{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\\
&-\sum_{s=0}^{3}4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{s}(\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{4-s}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi^{\kappa})(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j},\end{aligned}

(6.188)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{12,133}|&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+4\|\partial_{t}^{3}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi^{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+4\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi^{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+4\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega}\|J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi^{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{3}4\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{s}(\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E_{\kappa}^{\prime})+\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.189)

Similarly.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{12,132}&=-4\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{3}\partial_{t}\phi^{\kappa}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}|_{0}^{t}\\
&+4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\\
&+4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\\
&-\sum_{s=0}^{3}4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{s}(\partial_{t}\phi^{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{4-s}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j},\end{aligned}

(6.190)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{12,132}|&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+4\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+4\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+4\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega}\|J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{3}4\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{s}(\partial_{t}\phi^{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.191)

After integrating by part in time, a similar argument shows that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{12,131}&=-4\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{i}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}|_{0}^{t}+4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{i}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}+4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{i}J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{j}\\
&-4\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{s}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4-s}\partial_{t}v_{i}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j},\end{aligned}

(6.192)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{12,131}|&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+4\|J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v_{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+4\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v_{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+4\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v_{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{j}\|_{L6{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+4\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{4}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{t}v_{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(6.193)

Combining (
6.187
), (
6.189
), (
6.191
), (
6.193
), we have

\displaystyle|IV_{1213}|\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E_{\kappa}^{\prime})+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(6.194)

The estimate for
IV_{12,14}
is in a similar fashion. Integrating by part in time,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV_{12,14}&=4\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l}\partial_{t}^{3}q\partial_{l}((A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}|_{0}^{t}\\
&-4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{3}q\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l}\partial_{l}((A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}))\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\\
&-4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{3}qJ_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l}\partial_{l}((A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i})\partial_{t}^{4}v^{j}\\
&+4\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l})q\partial_{l}((A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\\
&+4\sum_{s=1}^{3}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{s}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l})\partial_{t}^{4-s}q\partial_{l}((A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i})\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j},\end{aligned}

(6.195)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV_{12,14}|&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+4\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{L^{6}(\Omega)}\|J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l}\partial_{l}((A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i})\|_{L^{3}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+4\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{L^{6}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l}\partial_{l}((A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i}))\|_{L^{3}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}((\Omega)}\\
&+4\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l}\partial_{l}((A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+4\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|q\partial_{l}((A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+4\sum_{s=1}^{3}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{s}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{l})\|_{L^{3}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4-s}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{l}((A^{\kappa})_{j}^{k}\partial_{k}v_{\kappa}^{i})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{3}v^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(6.196)

Hence, combining (
6.181
), (
6.183
), (
6.186
), (
6.194
) and (
6.196
), we have

\displaystyle|IV_{121}|\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E_{\kappa}^{\prime})+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(6.197)

Combining (
6.179
), (
6.180
) and (
6.197
), we have

\displaystyle|IV_{12}|\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E_{\kappa}^{\prime})+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(6.198)

Since
IV_{11,1}
has a similar structure as
IV_{12}
, we have

\displaystyle|IV_{11,1}|\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E_{\kappa}^{\prime})+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(6.199)

Combining (
6.178
), (
6.198
) and (
6.199
), we have

\displaystyle|IV_{1}|\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E_{\kappa}^{\prime})+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(6.200)

Combining (
6.109
), (
6.170
) and (
6.200
), we have

\displaystyle|IV|\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E_{\kappa}^{\prime})+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}q\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(6.201)

Now, invoking (
6.18
), choosing appropriate
\delta
,
we can write

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV|&\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E_{\kappa}^{\prime})+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(6.202)

It’s left for us to control
II
.

\displaystyle II=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}-\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{II_{1}}\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}-\partial_{t}^{4}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi^{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{II_{2}}

(6.203)

We expand
II_{1}
as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} II_{1}&=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}-J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{5}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}-\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{s}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{4-s}\phi^{\kappa}_{t}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}.\end{aligned}

(6.204)

From direct calculation, we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |II_{1}|&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\|J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{4}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\phi^{\kappa}_{t}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(6.205)

For
II_{2}
, we take its leading order as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} II_{2}&=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}-J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}-\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{s}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi^{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{4-s}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\\
&=\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{II_{21}}\underbrace{-\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{s}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{l}\partial_{t}^{4-s}\partial_{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{II_{22}}\\
&\underbrace{-\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{s}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi^{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{4-s}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{II_{23}}.\end{aligned}

(6.206)

The control of
II_{22}
and
II_{23}
reads

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |II_{22}|&\lesssim\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{4}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{l}\partial_{t}^{3-s}\partial_{l}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(6.207)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |II_{23}|&\lesssim\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{s}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi^{\kappa})\partial_{t}^{3-s}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.208)

Combining (
6.206
), (
6.207
), and (
6.208
), we have

\displaystyle II_{2}=\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}}_{II_{21}}+\mathcal{R},

(6.209)

where
\mathcal{R}\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})
. Combining (
6.203
), (
6.205
) and (
6.209
), we have

\displaystyle II=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}+\mathcal{R},

(6.210)

where
\mathcal{R}\lesssim\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))
.
Combining (
6.58
), (
6.61
), (
6.108
), (
6.202
), (
6.210
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}J_{k}|\partial_{t}^{4}v|^{2}+\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}\Pi^{\mu}_{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\Pi_{\alpha}^{\mu}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\cdot n^{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}+\mathcal{R},

(6.211)

where
|\mathcal{R}|\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E_{\kappa}^{\prime})+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi\|^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))
.
Now, combining (
4.59
) and (
6.211
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{\mu}\phi\\
&=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{j}\phi\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{i}\phi-\int_{\Omega}J_{k}|\partial_{t}^{4}v|^{2}\\
&-\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}\Pi^{\mu}_{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\Pi_{\alpha}^{\mu}-\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\cdot n^{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+\mathcal{R},\end{aligned}

(6.212)

where
|\mathcal{R}|\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E_{\kappa}^{\prime})+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi\|_{1}^{2}+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi\|^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))
.

Notice that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}v_{\kappa}^{\mu}\nabla^{\kappa}_{l}\phi\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{\mu}\phi|\\
\leq&|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\partial_{t}^{4}(v^{i}-v_{\kappa}^{i})|+|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}(\phi-\phi^{\kappa})\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}|\\
&+|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}(\phi-\phi^{\kappa})\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}|\\
+&|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\partial\Omega}(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{3}J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}(\phi^{\kappa}-\phi)\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}|+|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}(J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi^{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}|\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{l}J_{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{l}\partial_{t}^{4}(v^{i}-v_{\kappa}^{i})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\|J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}(\phi-\phi^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
+&\int_{0}^{t}\|J_{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}(\phi-\phi^{\kappa})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
+&\int_{0}^{t}\|(A^{\kappa})_{j}^{3}J_{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\partial\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\partial\Omega)}\|\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}(\phi^{\kappa}-\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}(\partial\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\partial\Omega)}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}+\int_{0}^{t}(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}+\int_{0}^{T}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\phi\|_{H^{4}(\Omega)}\|\kappa\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}\\
+&\int_{0}^{t}\kappa(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\|\partial_{t}^{4}\nabla^{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}\|\phi\|_{H^{4}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\\
\lesssim&\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))+(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}\\
\lesssim&\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))+\mathcal{P}(E_{\kappa}^{\prime}),\end{aligned}

(6.213)

where the last two steps we used (
4.56
). Hence, we have shown

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}g_{\kappa}^{ij}\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{j}\phi\partial_{t}^{4}\partial_{i}\phi+\int_{\Omega}J_{k}|\partial_{t}^{4}v|^{2}+\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\lambda}\Pi^{\mu}_{\lambda}\partial_{i}\partial_{t}^{3}v^{\alpha}\Pi_{\alpha}^{\mu}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\cdot n^{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E_{\kappa}^{\prime})+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}^{2}+\delta\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi\|_{1}^{2}+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi\|^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(6.214)

Now, we can show the estimate for
E_{\kappa}^{\prime}
in a similar way, but self-contained.

Lemma 6.9

(The estimate for the three time-differentiated
\kappa
-problem)
.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}\bar{\partial}v_{tt}^{\lambda}\Pi_{\lambda}^{\mu}\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}v_{tt}^{\alpha}\Pi_{\alpha}^{\mu}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}v_{ttt}\cdot n^{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}\\
&\lesssim C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))+(\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}v_{tt}\|_{H^{2}(\Omega)}^{2})+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}.\end{aligned}

(6.215)

Lemma 6.10

(The estimate for the two time-differentiated
\kappa
-problem)
.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}\bar{\partial}^{2}v_{t}^{\lambda}\Pi_{\lambda}^{\mu}\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}^{2}v_{t}^{\alpha}\Pi_{\alpha}^{\mu}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}^{2}v_{tt}\cdot n^{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}\\
&\lesssim C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))+(\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}^{2}v_{t}\|_{H^{2}(\Omega)}^{2})+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\partial}^{2}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}.\end{aligned}

(6.216)

Lemma 6.11

(The estimate for the time-differentiated
\kappa
-problem)
.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}\bar{\partial}^{3}v^{\lambda}\Pi_{\lambda}^{\mu}\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}^{3}v^{\alpha}\Pi_{\alpha}^{\mu}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}^{3}v_{t}\cdot n^{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}\\
&\lesssim C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))+(\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}^{3}v\|_{H^{2}(\Omega)}^{2})+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}\bar{\partial}^{3}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}.\end{aligned}

(6.217)

Lemma 6.12

(The estimate for the
\kappa
-problem)
.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}\bar{\partial}^{4}\eta^{\lambda}\Pi_{\lambda}^{\mu}\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\eta^{\alpha}\Pi_{\alpha}^{\mu}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}^{4}v\cdot n^{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}\\
&\lesssim C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}^{4}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}.\end{aligned}

(6.218)

Proof
.

Using the symbol
\bar{\partial}_{A}=\partial_{t}
or
\bar{\partial}
(at least one
\bar{\partial}
in
\bar{\partial}_{A}
here), multiplying
J_{\kappa}
on (
3.15
), and taking
\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}
then testing with
\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v
, we can write

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}(J_{\kappa}\partial_{t}v^{i})\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}}_{I^{\prime}}\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}q)\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}}_{II^{\prime}}\\
&=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}-\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}(J_{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}-\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi^{\kappa})\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}}_{III^{\prime}}.\end{aligned}

(6.219)

We can show that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |III^{\prime}|&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\|\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}(\bar{\partial}J_{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}(J_{\kappa}\bar{\partial}\phi^{\kappa}_{t}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\|\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}(J_{\kappa}\phi^{\kappa}_{t}\bar{\partial}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi^{\kappa})\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\Omega)}\|\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+\int_{0}^{t}\|\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}(J_{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\nabla^{\kappa}_{i}\phi^{\kappa}\nabla^{\kappa}_{j}\phi^{\kappa})\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}(\Omega)}\|\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}v^{i}\|_{H^{1.5}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.220)

For
I^{\prime}
, it’s evident that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} I^{\prime}&=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}\partial_{t}v^{i}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}+\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{4}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{s}\bar{\partial}_{A}J_{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3-s}\partial_{t}v^{i}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}\\
&=\underbrace{\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}|_{0}^{t}}_{I_{1}^{\prime}}\underbrace{+\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{4}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{s}\bar{\partial}_{A}\partial_{t}J_{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3-s}v^{i}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}J_{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}}_{I_{2}^{\prime}}.\end{aligned}

(6.221)

Since

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |I_{2}^{\prime}|&\lesssim\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{4}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|\bar{\partial}_{A}^{s}\bar{\partial}_{A}\partial_{t}J_{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3-s}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\|\partial_{t}J_{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(6.222)

we can express
I^{\prime}
as

\displaystyle I^{\prime}=\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}+\mathcal{R},

(6.223)

where
\mathcal{R}\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})
. Now, the estimate of
II^{\prime}
can be written as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} II^{\prime}&=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{3}q)\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}}_{II_{1}^{\prime}}\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j}q)\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}\partial_{j}v^{i}}_{II_{2}^{\prime}}.\end{aligned}

(6.224)

For the second term in (
6.224
) can be controlled as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} II_{2}^{\prime}&=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}q\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}\partial_{j}v^{i}+\sum_{s=1}^{3}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{s}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j})\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3-s}\bar{\partial}q\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}\partial_{j}v^{i}\\
&+\sum_{s=1}^{3}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{s}\bar{\partial}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j})\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3-s}q\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}\partial_{j}v^{i}\\
&=-\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}J_{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}q\bar{\partial}_{A}^{s}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{4-s}\partial_{j}v^{i}+\sum_{s=1}^{3}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{s}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j})\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3-s}\bar{\partial}q\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}\partial_{j}v^{i}\\
&+\sum_{s=1}^{3}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{s}\bar{\partial}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j})\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3-s}q\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}\partial_{j}v^{i}.\end{aligned}

(6.225)

Moreover,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |II_{2}^{\prime}|&\lesssim\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{4}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|J_{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}q\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\bar{\partial}_{A}^{s}\bar{\partial}_{A}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3-s}\partial_{j}v^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{4}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|\bar{\partial}_{A}^{s}\partial_{t}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j})\bar{\partial}_{A}^{2-s}\bar{\partial}q\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}(\Omega)}\|\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}\partial_{j}v^{i}\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\Omega)}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{4}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|\bar{\partial}_{A}^{s}\bar{\partial}_{A}\bar{\partial}(J_{\kappa}(A^{\kappa})_{i}^{j})\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3-s}q\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}(\Omega)}\|\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}\partial_{j}v^{i}\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\partial v\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}(\Omega)}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.226)

Now, the boundary term reads

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} II_{1}^{\prime}&=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}(-\sigma\sqrt{h}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n_{\kappa}n_{\kappa}^{i})\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}}_{II_{11}^{\prime}}\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}(\kappa\Delta_{0}(v\cdot n_{\kappa})n_{\kappa}^{i})\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{i}}_{II_{12}^{\prime}}\end{aligned}

(6.227)

The estimate is similar to
III_{1}
and
III_{2}
. Here we omit some details. (Replace
\partial_{t}^{4}
by
\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}
, every estimate is still valid.) We can write

\displaystyle II_{1}^{\prime}=\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}\eta^{\lambda}\Pi_{\lambda}^{\mu}\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}\eta^{\alpha}\Pi_{\alpha}^{\mu}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v\cdot n^{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+\mathcal{R},

(6.228)

where
\mathcal{R}\lesssim C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))+(\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}\eta\|_{H^{2}(\Omega)}^{2})+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}
. Thus, combining (
6.224
), (
6.226
) and (
6.228
), we have

\displaystyle II^{\prime}=\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}\eta^{\lambda}\Pi_{\lambda}^{\mu}\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}\eta^{\alpha}\Pi_{\alpha}^{\mu}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v\cdot n^{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+\mathcal{R},

(6.229)

where
\mathcal{R}\lesssim C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))+(\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}\eta\|_{H^{2}(\Omega)}^{2})+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}
. Hence, combining (
6.219
), (
6.220
), (
6.223
) and (
6.229
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\sigma\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}\eta^{\lambda}\Pi_{\lambda}^{\mu}\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}\eta^{\alpha}\Pi_{\alpha}^{\mu}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v\cdot n^{\kappa}\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}\\
&\lesssim C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))+(\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}\eta\|_{H^{2}(\Omega)}^{2})+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\bar{\partial}_{A}^{3}\bar{\partial}v\|_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}.\end{aligned}

(6.230)

Notice that
\sup\|\sqrt{\kappa}\eta\|_{6.5}
has been contained in energy. The lemmas follow immediately.

The proof of the following lemma is identical to that in
[
10
]
. Here, we simply provide the details regarding the dependence on the energy.

Lemma 6.13
.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\eta\cdot n^{\kappa}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta\cdot n_{\kappa}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\partial_{\gamma}\eta\cdot n^{\kappa}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta\cdot n_{\kappa}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{j}\eta^{\lambda})(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l})\lesssim\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\bar{\partial}^{6}\eta^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+C(\delta)T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.231)

Proof
.

We utilize the boundary condition of
q
:

\displaystyle\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}qn_{\kappa}^{l}=-\sigma(\sqrt{h}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n_{\kappa})n_{\kappa}^{l}+\kappa((1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n_{\kappa}))n_{\kappa}^{l}

(6.232)

Taking
\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}
on (
6.232
) and testing
h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}^{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}(\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}qn_{\kappa}^{l})h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{IV^{\prime}}&=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}-\sigma\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}(\sqrt{h}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n_{\kappa}n_{\kappa}^{l})h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{V^{\prime}}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}((1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n^{\kappa})n_{\kappa}^{l})h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{VI^{\prime}}.\end{aligned}

(6.233)

We analyze
V^{\prime}
first.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} V^{\prime}&=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}-\sigma\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}(\partial_{i}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}(\eta^{l})))h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{V_{1}^{\prime}}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}-\sigma\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}(\partial_{i}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}(\eta))\cdot(n_{\kappa}-n)n_{\kappa}^{l})h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{V_{2}^{\prime}}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}-\sigma\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}(\partial_{i}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}(\eta))\cdot n_{\kappa}(n_{\kappa}^{l}-n^{l})h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{V_{3}^{\prime}}\end{aligned}

(6.234)

Similar to
III
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} V_{1}^{\prime}&=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}-\sigma\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{i}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}(\delta^{l\lambda}-h^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{\beta}\eta^{l})(\partial_{\mu}\partial_{j}\eta^{\lambda}))h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{V_{11}^{\prime}}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}-\sigma\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{i}((h^{ij}h^{\alpha\beta}-h^{i\alpha}h^{j\beta})\partial_{\alpha}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{\beta\mu}^{2}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{l})h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{V_{12}^{\prime}}.\end{aligned}

(6.235)

V_{11}^{\prime}
will contribute a positive term as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} V_{11}^{\prime}&=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma\bar{\partial}^{4}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{j}\eta^{\lambda})\partial_{i}(h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l})\\
&=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma\bar{\partial}^{4}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{j}\eta^{\lambda})(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l})\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma\bar{\partial}^{4}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{j}\eta^{\lambda})\partial_{i}(h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\\
&=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{j}\eta^{\lambda})(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l})}_{V_{11,1}^{\prime}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma\bar{\partial}^{s}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\Pi_{\lambda}^{l})(\bar{\partial}^{4-s}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{j}\eta^{\lambda})(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l})}_{V_{11,2}^{\prime}}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma\bar{\partial}^{4}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{j}\eta^{\lambda})\partial_{i}(h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}}_{V_{11,3}^{\prime}}.\end{aligned}

(6.236)

The lower orders can be estimated as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |V_{11,2}^{\prime}|&\lesssim\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{4}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\sigma\|\bar{\partial}^{s}\bar{\partial}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\Pi_{\lambda}^{l})(\bar{\partial}^{3-s}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{j}\eta^{\lambda})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|h^{\mu\nu}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+C(\delta)T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(6.237)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} V_{11,3}^{\prime}&=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{j}\eta^{\lambda})\partial_{i}(h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\\
&+\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma\bar{\partial}^{s}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij})\bar{\partial}^{4-s}(\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{j}\eta^{\lambda})\partial_{i}(h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k},\end{aligned}

(6.238)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |V_{11,3}^{\prime}|&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\sigma(\|\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{j}\eta^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{i}(h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{4}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\sigma\|\bar{\partial}^{s}\bar{\partial}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij})\bar{\partial}^{3-s}(\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{j}\eta^{\lambda})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{i}(h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{j}\eta^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+C(\delta)T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(6.239)

after application of Young’s inequality. Combining (
6.236
), (
6.237
) and (
6.239
), we have

\displaystyle V_{11}^{\prime}=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{j}\eta^{\lambda})(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l})}_{V_{11,1}^{\prime}}+\mathcal{R},

(6.240)

where
|\mathcal{R}|\lesssim\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\bar{\partial}^{6}\eta^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+C(\delta)T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})
. Now, the estimate of
V_{12}^{\prime}
needs the following geometric relationship on boundary.

\displaystyle\bar{\partial}_{k}\eta^{i}\Pi_{i}^{l}=0,\quad\textit{$k=1$ or $2$.}

(6.241)

Utilizing this property, we can express
V_{12}^{\prime}
as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} V_{12}^{\prime}&=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma\bar{\partial}^{4}((h^{ij}h^{\alpha\beta}-h^{i\alpha}h^{j\beta})\partial_{\alpha}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{\beta\mu}^{2}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{l})h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma\bar{\partial}^{4}((h^{ij}h^{\alpha\beta}-h^{i\alpha}h^{j\beta})\partial_{\alpha}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{\beta\mu}^{2}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{l})\partial_{i}(h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\\
&=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma(h^{ij}h^{\alpha\beta}-h^{i\alpha}h^{j\beta})\partial_{\alpha}\eta^{\lambda}\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{\beta\mu}^{2}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{V_{12,1}^{\prime}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma\bar{\partial}^{s}((h^{ij}h^{\alpha\beta}-h^{i\alpha}h^{j\beta})\partial_{\alpha}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{l})\bar{\partial}^{4-s}(\partial_{\beta\mu}^{2}\eta^{\lambda})h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{V_{12,2}^{\prime}}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma\bar{\partial}^{4}((h^{ij}h^{\alpha\beta}-h^{i\alpha}h^{j\beta})\partial_{\alpha}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{\beta\mu}^{2}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{l})\partial_{i}(h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}}_{V_{12,3}^{\prime}}.\end{aligned}

(6.242)

Notice that
V_{12,1}^{\prime}=0
. The rest of two terms can be controlled as:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |V_{12,2}^{\prime}|&\lesssim\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{4}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\sigma\|\bar{\partial}^{s}\bar{\partial}((h^{ij}h^{\alpha\beta}-h^{i\alpha}h^{j\beta})\partial_{\alpha}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{l})\bar{\partial}^{3-s}(\partial_{\beta\mu}^{2}\eta^{\lambda})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|h^{\mu\nu}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+C(\delta)T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(6.243)

and using
\Pi_{k}^{l}=\Pi_{\gamma}^{l}\Pi_{k}^{\gamma}
,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} V_{12,3}^{\prime}&=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma(h^{ij}h^{\alpha\beta}-h^{i\alpha}h^{j\beta})\partial_{\alpha}\eta^{\lambda}\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{\beta\mu}^{2}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{l}\partial_{i}(h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\\
&+\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma\bar{\partial}^{s}((h^{ij}h^{\alpha\beta}-h^{i\alpha}h^{j\beta})\partial_{\alpha}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{l})\bar{\partial}^{4-s}\partial_{\beta\mu}^{2}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{i}(h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\\
&=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma(h^{ij}h^{\alpha\beta}-h^{i\alpha}h^{j\beta})\partial_{\alpha}\eta^{\lambda}\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{\beta\mu}^{2}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{i}\Pi_{\gamma}^{l}\Pi_{k}^{\gamma}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\\
&+\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma\bar{\partial}^{s}((h^{ij}h^{\alpha\beta}-h^{i\alpha}h^{j\beta})\partial_{\alpha}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{l})\bar{\partial}^{4-s}\partial_{\beta\mu}^{2}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{i}(h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\\
&=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma(h^{ij}h^{\alpha\beta}-h^{i\alpha}h^{j\beta})\partial_{\alpha}\eta^{\lambda}\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{\mu}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{i}\Pi_{\gamma}^{l}\Pi_{k}^{\gamma}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\beta\nu}^{2}\eta^{k}\\
&-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{\mu}\eta^{\lambda}\bar{\partial}_{\beta}((h^{ij}h^{\alpha\beta}-h^{i\alpha}h^{j\beta})\partial_{\alpha}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{i}\Pi_{\gamma}^{l}\Pi_{k}^{\gamma})\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\\
&+\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{4}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma\bar{\partial}^{s}((h^{ij}h^{\alpha\beta}-h^{i\alpha}h^{j\beta})\partial_{\alpha}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{l})\bar{\partial}^{4-s}\partial_{\beta\mu}^{2}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{i}(h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k},\end{aligned}

(6.244)

where we used the orthogonal relationship again. Now, we can show that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |V_{12,3}^{\prime}|&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\sigma\|(h^{ij}h^{\alpha\beta}-h^{i\alpha}h^{j\beta})\partial_{\alpha}\eta^{\lambda}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{\mu}\eta^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{j}\eta^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{i}\Pi_{\gamma}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\Pi_{k}^{\gamma}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\beta\nu}^{2}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\sigma\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{\mu}\eta^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}_{\beta}((h^{ij}h^{\alpha\beta}-h^{i\alpha}h^{j\beta})\partial_{\alpha}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{i}\Pi_{\gamma}^{l}\Pi_{k}^{\gamma})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{4}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\sigma\|\bar{\partial}^{s}\bar{\partial}((h^{ij}h^{\alpha\beta}-h^{i\alpha}h^{j\beta})\partial_{\alpha}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{j}\eta^{l})\bar{\partial}^{3-s}\partial_{\beta\mu}^{2}\eta^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{i}(h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\beta\nu}^{2}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+C(\delta)T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\end{aligned}

(6.245)

Combining (
6.242
), (
6.243
) and (
6.245
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |V_{12}^{\prime}|\lesssim\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\bar{\partial}^{6}\eta^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+C(\delta)T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.246)

Combining (
6.235
), (
6.240
) and (
6.246
), we have

\displaystyle V_{1}^{\prime}=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{j}\eta^{\lambda})(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l})+\mathcal{R},

(6.247)

where
|\mathcal{R}|\lesssim\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\bar{\partial}^{6}\eta^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+C(\delta)T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})
. Now,

V_{2}^{\prime}
and
V_{3}^{\prime}
can be estimated in a similar fashion. We show
V_{2}^{\prime}
as an example. With a little bit abuse of notation, we will suppress the index if it is not important in our analysis.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} V_{2}^{\prime}&\sim+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}-\sigma\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\partial_{i}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}(\eta^{\lambda}))(n_{\kappa}^{\lambda}-n^{\lambda})n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}-\sigma\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{i}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}(\eta^{\lambda}))\bar{\partial}((n_{\kappa}^{\lambda}-n^{\lambda})n_{\kappa}^{l})h^{.\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}\\
&\sim+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}(\eta^{\lambda}))(n_{\kappa}^{\lambda}-n^{\lambda})n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\partial_{i}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}(\eta^{\lambda}))\partial_{i}(n_{\kappa}^{\lambda}-n^{\lambda})n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}(\eta^{\lambda}))(n_{\kappa}^{\lambda}-n^{\lambda})\partial_{i}(n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}-\sigma\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{i}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}(\eta^{\lambda}))\bar{\partial}((n_{\kappa}^{\lambda}-n^{\lambda})n_{\kappa}^{l})h^{.\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}\end{aligned}

(6.248)

Notice that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\bar{\partial}^{5}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\partial_{j}(\eta^{\lambda}))\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}&\leq\|\bar{\partial}^{4}(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}(\delta^{\alpha\lambda}-h^{kl}\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{l}\eta^{\alpha})\bar{\partial}\partial_{j}\eta^{\alpha}))\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\|\bar{\partial}^{4}(\sqrt{h}(h^{ij}h^{kl}-h^{jl}h^{ik})\partial_{j}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{k}\eta^{\lambda}\partial_{l}\bar{\partial}\eta^{\lambda})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\eta\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})},\end{aligned}

(6.249)

\displaystyle\|n-n^{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}+\|\bar{\partial}(n-n^{\kappa})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\lesssim\sqrt{\kappa}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.250)

V_{2}^{\prime}
can clearly be bounded by

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |V_{2}^{\prime}|&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{\kappa}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\eta\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{6}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\\
&\lesssim\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\bar{\partial}^{6}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).\end{aligned}

(6.251)

Similarly,

\displaystyle|V_{3}^{\prime}|\lesssim\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\bar{\partial}^{6}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+C(\delta)(\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})).

(6.252)

Combining (
6.234
), (
6.247
), (
6.251
) and (
6.252
), we have

\displaystyle V^{\prime}=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{j}\eta^{\lambda})(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l})+\mathcal{R},

(6.253)

where
|\mathcal{R}|\lesssim\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\bar{\partial}^{6}\eta^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+C(\delta)T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})
. The estimate for
IV^{\prime}
is quite direct.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} IV^{\prime}&=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\bar{\partial}^{4}(\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}qn_{\kappa}^{l})h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}\\
&-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\bar{\partial}^{4}(\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}qn_{\kappa}^{l})\bar{\partial}_{\mu}(h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\end{aligned}

(6.254)

Since
\|q\|_{4.5}\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |IV^{\prime}|&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}(\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}qn_{\kappa}^{l})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|h^{\mu\nu}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}(\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}qn_{\kappa}^{l})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}_{\mu}(h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\bar{\partial}^{6}\eta^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+C(\delta)T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.255)

Now, we analyze
VI^{\prime}
, which will give us the boundary regularity of
\sqrt{\kappa}\eta
.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} VI^{\prime}&=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}(1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n^{\kappa})n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{VI_{1}^{\prime}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{s=0}^{4}C_{5}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{s}(1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n^{\kappa})(\bar{\partial}^{5-s}n_{\kappa}^{l})h^{.\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{VI_{2}^{\prime}}.\end{aligned}

(6.256)

We estimate
VI_{2}^{\prime}
first.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} VI_{2}^{\prime}&=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa(1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n^{\kappa})(\bar{\partial}^{5}n_{\kappa}^{l})h^{.\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{VI_{21}^{\prime}}\underbrace{+\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{5}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{s}(1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n^{\kappa})(\bar{\partial}^{5-s}n_{\kappa}^{l})h^{.\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{VI_{22}^{\prime}},\end{aligned}

(6.257)

we control
VI_{21}^{\prime}
as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |VI_{21}^{\prime}|&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|(1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|(\bar{\partial}^{5}n_{\kappa}^{l})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|h^{.\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\eta\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.258)

For
VI_{22}^{\prime}
, we can write

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} VI_{22}^{\prime}&=\underbrace{\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{5}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{s}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})(\bar{\partial}^{5-s}n_{\kappa}^{l})h^{.\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{V_{22,1}^{\prime}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{5}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{s}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}(\bar{\partial}^{5-s}n_{\kappa}^{l})h^{.\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{V_{22,2}^{\prime}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{5}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{s}\partial_{\gamma}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})(\bar{\partial}^{5-s}n_{\kappa}^{l})h^{.\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{VI_{22,3}^{\prime}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{5}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{s}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})(\bar{\partial}^{5-s}n_{\kappa}^{l})\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}(h^{.\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}}_{V_{22,4}^{\prime}}.\end{aligned}

(6.259)

A term by term estimate shows that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |VI_{22,1}^{\prime}|&\lesssim\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{5}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\bar{\partial}^{s}\bar{\partial}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})(\bar{\partial}^{4-s}n_{\kappa}^{l})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|h^{.\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(6.260)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |VI_{22,2}^{\prime}|&\lesssim C_{5}^{1}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\bar{\partial}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}(\bar{\partial}^{4}n_{\kappa}^{l})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|h^{.\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{1}C_{5}^{s+2}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\bar{\partial}^{s+2}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})(\bar{\partial}^{1-s}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}^{2}n_{\kappa}^{l})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|h^{.\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+C_{5}^{4}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}(\bar{\partial}n_{\kappa}^{l})h^{.\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\eta\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})}+\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa(\|v\|_{H^{5}(\Gamma_{1})}+\|\eta\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})})\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(6.261)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |VI_{22,3}^{\prime}|&\lesssim C_{5}^{4}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{\gamma}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|(\bar{\partial}n_{\kappa}^{l})h^{.\nu}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{5}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\bar{\partial}^{s}\bar{\partial}\partial_{\gamma}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})(\bar{\partial}^{2-s}\bar{\partial}^{2}n_{\kappa}^{l})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|h^{.\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(6.262)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |VI_{22,4}^{\prime}|&\lesssim\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{5}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\bar{\partial}^{s}\bar{\partial}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})(\bar{\partial}^{4-s}n_{\kappa}^{l})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}(h^{.\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.263)

Combining
(
6.259
), (
6.260
), (
6.261
), (
6.262
) and (
6.263
), we have

\displaystyle|VI_{22}^{\prime}|\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.264)

Combining (
6.257
), (
6.258
) and (
6.264
), we have

\displaystyle|VI_{2}^{\prime}|\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.265)

Now, we estimate the major part of
VI^{\prime}
as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} VI_{1}^{\prime}&=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\partial_{\gamma}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\partial_{\gamma}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}(n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\\
&=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}v\cdot n^{\kappa}n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\partial_{\gamma}v\cdot n^{\kappa}n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{VI_{11}^{\prime}}\\
&\underbrace{+\sum_{s=0}^{4}C_{5}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{s}v\cdot\bar{\partial}^{5-s}n^{\kappa}n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{.\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}+\sum_{s=1}^{4}C_{6}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{s}v\cdot\bar{\partial}^{6-s}n^{\kappa}n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\nu}\bar{\partial}^{5}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{VI_{12}^{\prime}}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\partial_{\gamma}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}(n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}}_{VI_{13}^{\prime}}\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa v^{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}n_{\kappa}^{i}n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{VI_{14}^{\prime}}.\end{aligned}

(6.266)

The estimate of
VI_{12}^{\prime}
is similar to the estimate of
VI_{2}^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |VI_{12}^{\prime}|&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|v\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{5}n^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{.\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{3}C_{5}^{s+1}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\bar{\partial}^{s+1}v\cdot\bar{\partial}^{4-s}n^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{.\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+6\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\bar{\partial}v\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{5}n^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{5}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{6}^{s+2}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\bar{\partial}^{s+2}v\cdot\bar{\partial}^{4-s}n^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{5}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\eta\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.267)

For
VI_{13}^{\prime}
, we can show that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} VI_{13}^{\prime}&=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{\gamma}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}(n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{\gamma}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}(n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}.\end{aligned}

(6.268)

Thus,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |VI_{13}^{\prime}|&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{\gamma}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}(n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{\gamma}(v\cdot n^{\kappa})\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}(n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\kappa(\|v\|_{H^{5}}+\|\eta\|_{H^{6}})\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\eta\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.269)

Now, we estimate the hardest term
VI_{14}^{\prime}
. Writing
v^{i}=v\cdot n^{\kappa}n_{\kappa}^{i}+v\cdot\frac{\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}^{i}+v\cdot\frac{\partial_{2}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{2}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}\partial_{2}\eta_{\kappa}^{i}
, utilizing
n_{\kappa}^{i}n_{\kappa}^{i}=1
and
n_{\kappa}^{i}\bar{\partial}_{k}\eta^{i}=0
,
k=1,2
we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} VI_{14}^{\prime}&=\underbrace{-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{s=1}^{5}C_{6}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa v\cdot n^{\kappa}\bar{\partial}^{s}n_{\kappa}^{i}\bar{\partial}^{6-s}n_{\kappa}^{i}n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{.\nu}\bar{\partial}^{5}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{VI_{14,1}^{\prime}}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa v\cdot\frac{\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}^{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}n_{\kappa}^{i}n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{VI_{14,2}^{\prime}}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa v\cdot\frac{\partial_{2}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{2}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}\partial_{2}\eta_{\kappa}^{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}n_{\kappa}^{i}n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{VI_{14,3}^{\prime}}.\end{aligned}

(6.270)

From direct calculation,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |VI_{14,1}^{\prime}|&\lesssim 6\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|v\cdot n^{\kappa}\bar{\partial}n_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{5}n_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{.\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{5}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{s=0}^{2}C_{6}^{s+2}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|v\cdot n^{\kappa}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{s}\bar{\partial}^{2}n_{\kappa}^{i}\bar{\partial}^{4-s}n_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{.\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{5}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\eta\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\eta\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.271)

While the structure of
VI_{14,2}^{\prime}
and
VI_{14,3}^{\prime}
is the same. It suffices to show
VI_{14,2}^{\prime}
as an example.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} VI_{14,2}^{\prime}&=\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa v\cdot\frac{\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}^{i}n_{\kappa}^{i}n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{VI_{14,21}^{\prime}}\\
&\underbrace{-\sum_{s=1}^{5}C_{6}^{s}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa v\cdot\frac{\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}\bar{\partial}^{s}\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}^{i}\bar{\partial}^{6-s}n_{\kappa}^{i}n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{.\nu}\bar{\partial}^{5}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l}}_{VI_{14,22}^{\prime}}.\end{aligned}

(6.272)

For
VI_{14,22}^{\prime}
, we can estimate it as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |VI_{14,22}^{\prime}|&\lesssim 6\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|v\cdot\frac{\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}\bar{\partial}\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{5}n_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{.\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{5}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+6\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|v\cdot\frac{\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{5}\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}n_{\kappa}^{i}n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{.\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{5}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\sum_{s=0}^{4}C_{6}^{s+2}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|v\cdot\frac{\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{s}\bar{\partial}^{2}\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}^{i}\bar{\partial}^{4-s}n_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{.\nu}\Pi_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{5}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.273)

For
VI_{14,21}^{\prime}
, we first write it into a more symmetric form:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} VI_{14,21}^{\prime}&=\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa v\cdot\frac{\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}^{i}n_{\kappa}^{i}n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}n_{\kappa}^{k}n_{\kappa}^{l}}_{VI_{14,211}^{\prime}}\\
&\underbrace{-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa v\cdot\frac{\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}^{i}n_{\kappa}^{i}n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}(\Pi_{k}^{l}-(\Pi_{\kappa})_{k}^{l})}_{VI_{14,212}^{\prime}}.\end{aligned}

(6.274)

The second term
VI_{14,212}
can be controlled by a standard
\kappa\|f\|_{H^{s+1}(\Gamma_{1})}\lesssim\|f\|_{s}
. Since
\Pi=n\otimes n
, and we have analyzed that
