can be viewed as a smooth function of
\bar{\partial}\eta
. Thus, we have

\displaystyle\|\Pi_{k}^{l}-(\Pi_{\kappa})_{k}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\lesssim\kappa\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\partial\eta\|_{H^{2.5}(\Gamma_{1})}.

(6.275)

Now, a standard
L^{2}
estimate gives us

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |VI_{14,212}^{\prime}|&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|v\cdot\frac{\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|n_{\kappa}^{i}n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|(\Pi_{k}^{l}-(\Pi_{\kappa})_{k}^{l})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\eta\|_{H^{7}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\eta\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})}\kappa\|\eta\|_{H^{3.5}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\eta\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}\\
&\lesssim T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.276)

For
VI_{14,211}^{\prime}
, we utilize the double horizontal convolution,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} VI_{14,211}^{\prime}&=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\partial_{1}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta^{i}\Lambda_{\kappa}(v\cdot\frac{\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}n_{\kappa}^{i}n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}n_{\kappa}^{k}n_{\kappa}^{l})\\
&=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\partial_{1}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta^{i}(v\cdot\frac{\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}n_{\kappa}^{i}h^{\mu\nu}n_{\kappa}^{k}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta^{k})\\
&-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\partial_{1}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta^{i}[\Lambda_{\kappa},(v\cdot\frac{\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}n_{\kappa}^{i}h^{\mu\nu}n_{\kappa}^{k})]\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\\
&=\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta^{i}\partial_{1}(v\cdot\frac{\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}n_{\kappa}^{i}h^{\mu\nu}n_{\kappa}^{k})\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta^{k}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta^{i}[\Lambda_{\kappa},\partial_{1}(v\cdot\frac{\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}n_{\kappa}^{i}h^{\mu\nu}n_{\kappa}^{k}]\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k})\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta^{i}[\Lambda_{\kappa},(v\cdot\frac{\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}n_{\kappa}^{i}h^{\mu\nu}n_{\kappa}^{k}]\partial_{1}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}).\end{aligned}

(6.277)

Invoking (
2.21
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |VI_{14,211}^{\prime}|&\lesssim\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{1}(v\cdot\frac{\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}n_{\kappa}^{i}h^{\mu\nu}n_{\kappa}^{k})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|[\Lambda_{\kappa},\partial_{1}(v\cdot\frac{\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}n_{\kappa}^{i}h^{\mu\nu}n_{\kappa}^{k})]\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|[\Lambda_{\kappa},(v\cdot\frac{\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}n_{\kappa}^{i}h^{\mu\nu}n_{\kappa}^{k})]\partial_{1}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\eta\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})}\|(v\cdot\frac{\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}}{|\partial_{1}\eta_{\kappa}|^{2}}n_{\kappa}^{i}h^{\mu\nu}n_{\kappa}^{k})\|_{H^{2.5}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\eta\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.278)

Combining (
6.274
), (
6.276
) and (
6.276
), we have

\displaystyle|VI_{14,21}^{\prime}|\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.279)

Combining (
6.272
), (
6.273
) and (
6.279
), we have

\displaystyle|VI_{14,2}^{\prime}|\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.280)

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle|VI_{14,3}^{\prime}|\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.281)

Combining (
6.270
), (
6.271
), (
6.280
) and (
6.281
), we have

\displaystyle|VI_{14}^{\prime}|\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.282)

Now, we focus on
VI_{11}^{\prime}
. Using a similar strategy, we write it into a symmetric form:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} VI_{11}^{\prime}&=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}v\cdot n^{\kappa}n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}n_{\kappa}^{k}n_{\kappa}^{l}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\partial_{\gamma}v\cdot n^{\kappa}n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}n_{\kappa}^{k}n_{\kappa}^{l}}_{VI_{11,1}^{\prime}}\\
&\underbrace{+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}v\cdot n^{\kappa}n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}(\Pi_{k}^{l}-(\Pi_{\kappa})_{k}^{l})+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\partial_{\gamma}v\cdot n^{\kappa}n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}(\Pi_{k}^{l}-(\Pi_{\kappa})_{k}^{l})}_{VI_{11,2}^{\prime}}.\end{aligned}

(6.283)

For
VI_{11,2}^{\prime}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |VI_{11,2}^{\prime}|&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}v\cdot n^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|(\Pi_{k}^{l}-(\Pi_{\kappa})_{k}^{l})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\partial_{\gamma}v\cdot n^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|n_{\kappa}^{l}h^{\mu\nu}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|(\Pi_{k}^{l}-(\Pi_{\kappa})_{k}^{l})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\kappa^{2}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\partial_{\gamma}v\cdot n^{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\eta\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\eta\|_{H^{3.5}(\Gamma_{1})}.\end{aligned}

(6.284)

Now, we solve the elliptic problem:

\displaystyle\kappa((1-\bar{\Delta})(v\cdot n_{\kappa}))n_{\kappa}^{l}=\sigma(\sqrt{h}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n_{\kappa})n_{\kappa}^{l}-\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}qn_{\kappa}^{l},

(6.285)

\displaystyle\|\kappa v\cdot n_{\kappa}\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})}\lesssim\|\sigma(\sqrt{h}\Delta_{h}(\eta)\cdot n_{\kappa})n_{\kappa}^{l}\|_{H^{4}(\Gamma_{1})}+\|\sqrt{h_{\kappa}}qn_{\kappa}^{l}\|_{H^{4}(\Gamma_{1})}\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\eta\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})}+\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.286)

Therefore,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\kappa\bar{\partial}^{6}v\cdot n_{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}&\lesssim\|\kappa v\cdot n_{\kappa}\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})}+\sum_{s=0}^{5}\|\kappa\bar{\partial}^{s}v\cdot\bar{\partial}^{6-s}n_{\kappa}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\eta\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})}+\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+\|\kappa v\|_{H^{5}(\Gamma_{1})}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+\kappa\|v\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\eta^{\kappa}\|_{H^{7}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\eta\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})}+\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+\|\kappa v\|_{H^{5}(\Gamma_{1})}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.287)

Now, we can write

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |VI_{11,2}^{\prime}|&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})(\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})\|\eta\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})}+\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})+\|\kappa v\|_{H^{5}(\Gamma_{1})}\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}))\|\eta\|_{H^{6}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\eta\|_{H^{3.5}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).\end{aligned}

(6.288)

The term
VI_{11,1}^{\prime}
provide us with the desired estimate:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} VI_{11,1}^{\prime}&=\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\eta\cdot n^{\kappa}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta\cdot n_{\kappa}|_{0}^{t}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\partial_{\gamma}\eta\cdot n^{\kappa}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta\cdot n_{\kappa}|_{0}^{t}\\
&-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}v^{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\partial_{t}(n_{\kappa}^{i}h^{\mu\nu}n_{\kappa}^{k})-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\partial_{\gamma}\eta^{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\partial_{t}(n_{\kappa}^{i}h^{\mu\nu}n_{\kappa}^{k}).\end{aligned}

(6.289)

Since

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &|-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}v^{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\partial_{t}(n_{\kappa}^{i}h^{\mu\nu}n_{\kappa}^{k})-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\partial_{\gamma}\eta^{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\partial_{t}(n_{\kappa}^{i}h^{\mu\nu}n_{\kappa}^{k})|\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\eta^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}(n_{\kappa}^{i}h^{\mu\nu}n_{\kappa}^{k})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\partial_{\gamma}\eta^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}\|\partial_{t}(n_{\kappa}^{i}h^{\mu\nu}n_{\kappa}^{k})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Gamma_{1})}\\
&\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}),\end{aligned}

(6.290)

we arrive at

\displaystyle VI_{11,1}^{\prime}=\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\eta\cdot n^{\kappa}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta\cdot n_{\kappa}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\partial_{\gamma}\eta\cdot n^{\kappa}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta\cdot n_{\kappa}+\mathcal{R},

(6.291)

where
|\mathcal{R}|\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})
. Combining (
6.283
), (
6.288
) and (
6.291
), we have

\displaystyle VI_{11}^{\prime}=\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\eta\cdot n^{\kappa}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta\cdot n_{\kappa}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\partial_{\gamma}\eta\cdot n^{\kappa}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta\cdot n_{\kappa}+\mathcal{R},

(6.292)

where

. Combining (

), (

), (

), (

\displaystyle VI_{1}^{\prime}=\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\eta\cdot n^{\kappa}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta\cdot n_{\kappa}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\partial_{\gamma}\eta\cdot n^{\kappa}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta\cdot n_{\kappa}+\mathcal{R},

(6.293)

where

.
Combining (

\displaystyle VI^{\prime}=\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\eta\cdot n^{\kappa}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta\cdot n_{\kappa}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\partial_{\gamma}\eta\cdot n^{\kappa}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta\cdot n_{\kappa}+\mathcal{R},

(6.294)

where

. Combining (

),(

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\eta\cdot n^{\kappa}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta\cdot n_{\kappa}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\kappa\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\mu}\partial_{\gamma}\eta\cdot n^{\kappa}h^{\mu\nu}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\gamma}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta\cdot n_{\kappa}\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Gamma_{1}}\sigma(\sqrt{h}h^{ij}\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\bar{\partial}^{4}\partial_{\mu}\partial_{j}\eta^{\lambda})(h^{\mu\nu}\partial_{i}\bar{\partial}^{4}\bar{\partial}_{\nu}\eta^{k}\Pi_{k}^{l})=\mathcal{R},\end{aligned}

(6.295)

where
|\mathcal{R}|\lesssim\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|\Pi_{\lambda}^{l}\bar{\partial}^{6}\eta^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{1})}^{2}+C(\delta)T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa})
.

Now, we check that we have finished all energy estimate. Combining

(6.296)

Inductively, combining (

(6.297)

\displaystyle\sup_{t}\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1.5}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi\|_{1}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\sqrt{\kappa}\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{1.5}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+T\mathcal{P}(E^{\kappa}).

(6.299)

Invoking the Grönwall inequality, we prove the uniform in

estimate.

7
A posteriori estimate

First, as indicated by the estimate (
4.40
), we have:

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{k}\phi\in L_{t}^{\infty}H^{5.5-k},\quad k=0,1,2,3.

(7.1)

Applying a similar scheme as above, we can mollify the initial data and establish the solution in higher-order terms. Thus, it suffices to provide the a priori estimates below to improve the regularity, after which a standard approximation scheme follows.

With this, in
[
10
]
, the following improvement scheme is established:
We differentiate the boundary equation
qn_{i}=-\sigma\Delta_{h}(\eta^{i})
with
\bar{\partial}_{A}
, then it becomes

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{3}qn_{i}=\Delta_{h}(v_{tt})+l.o.t.

(7.2)

The elliptic estimate with curl and divergence analysis will give us

\displaystyle\|v_{tt}\|_{H^{3}(\Omega)}\leq M_{0}.

(7.3)

Similarly, using
\|q_{t}\|_{3.5}\leq M_{0}
and
\|q_{tt}\|_{2.5}\leq M_{0}
we will obtain

\displaystyle\|v\|_{H^{5.5}(\Omega)}+\|v_{t}\|_{H^{4.5}(\Omega)}\leq M_{0}.

(7.4)

This will leads to the improvement of regularity of
q
via elliptic estimate with Neumann boundary:

\displaystyle\|q\|_{H^{5}(\Omega)}\leq M_{0}+\|v_{t}\|_{H^{3.5}(\Gamma)}\leq M_{0}.

(7.5)

Now, we rewrite moving boundary as a graph of function
S:\mathbb{T}^{2}\to\mathbb{R}
. Utilizing an identical method in
[
10
]
, we can obtain
In our case, the same energy estimate structure gives us

\displaystyle\|S\|_{H^{6.5}}\leq M_{0}.

(7.6)

8
Uniqueness of the solution

Suppose that
(\eta^{1},v^{1},q^{1},\phi^{1})
and
(\eta^{2},v^{2},q^{2},\phi^{2})
are both solutions of (
1.7
). We define

\displaystyle w=v^{1}-v^{2},\quad r=q^{1}-q^{2},\quad\xi=\eta^{1}-\eta^{2},\quad\textit{and }z=\phi^{1}-\phi^{2}.

Then it follows that

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\partial_{t}r&=w\quad\textit{in $\Omega\times[0,T]$},\\
\partial_{t}w_{i}+(A^{1})_{i}^{j}\partial_{j}r&=(A^{2}-A^{1})_{i}^{j}\partial_{j}q^{2}-(A^{1})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}((A^{1})_{k}^{l}\partial_{l}z\odot(A^{1})_{i}^{m}\partial_{m}d^{1})\\
&-(A^{1})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}((A^{1})_{k}^{l}\partial_{l}d^{2}\odot(A^{1})_{i}^{m}z)-(A^{1}-A^{2})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}((A^{1})_{k}^{l}\partial_{l}d^{2}\odot(A^{1})_{i}^{m}\partial_{m}d^{2})\quad\textit{in $\Omega\times[0,T]$,}\\
&-(A^{2})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}((A^{1}-A^{2})_{k}^{l}\partial_{l}d^{2}\odot(A^{1})_{i}^{m}\partial_{m}d^{2})-(A^{2})_{k}^{j}\partial_{j}((A^{2})_{k}^{l}\partial_{l}d^{2}\odot(A^{1}-A^{2})_{i}^{m}\partial_{m}d^{2})\\
(A^{1})_{i}^{j}\partial_{j}w^{i}&=(A^{2}-A^{1})_{i}^{j}v_{j}^{i}\quad\textit{in $\Omega\times[0,T]$,}\\
\partial_{t}z-\Delta_{1}z&=(A^{2}-A^{1})_{i}^{j}\partial_{j}((A^{1})_{i}^{k}\partial_{k}d^{2})+(A^{2})_{i}^{j}\partial_{j}((A^{2}-A^{1})_{i}^{k}\partial_{k}d^{2})\\
&+z|\nabla_{A^{1}}d^{1}|^{2}+d^{2}((A^{1})_{i}^{k}\partial_{k}z\cdot(A^{1})_{i}^{l}\partial_{l}d^{1})\quad\textit{in $\Omega\times[0,T]$},\\
&+d^{2}((A^{1})_{i}^{k}\partial_{k}d^{2}\cdot(A^{1})_{i}^{l}\partial_{l}z)+d^{2}((A^{1}-A^{2})_{i}^{k}\partial_{k}d^{2}\cdot(A^{1})_{i}^{l}\partial_{l}d^{2})\\
&+d^{2}((A^{2})_{i}^{k}\partial_{k}d^{2}\cdot(A^{1}-A^{2})_{i}^{l}\partial_{l}d^{2})\\
rn_{1}&=-\sigma\Pi^{1}h^{1\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}\xi-\sigma\sqrt{h^{1}}\Delta_{h^{1}-h^{2}}(\eta^{2}),\quad\textit{on $\Gamma\times[0,T]$,}\\
g_{1}^{3i}\partial_{i}z&=(g_{2}^{3i}-g_{1}^{3i})\partial_{i}\phi^{2}\quad\textit{on $\Gamma\times[0,T]$,}(\xi,w,z)=(0,0,0)\quad\textit{on $\Omega\times\{t=0\}$}.\end{cases}

(8.1)

We define

\displaystyle H(t)=\sum_{k=0}^{3}(\|\partial_{t}^{k}w\|_{4.5-k}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{k}z\|_{5.5-k}^{2})

(8.2)

It suffices to prove
H(t)\lesssim T\mathcal{P}(H)
. From our previous argument, we can set
E^{1}+E^{2}\leq M_{0}
. (
E^{1}
and
E^{2}
are the energy defined in Section 4) The basic idea is still to give a
curl
,
div
, and boundary estimate. The form is more complicated than original equation, but the proof is similar. To show this, when we give
curl
,
div
estimate, we first write it in the form of
L_{j}\eta^{1}=...
and
L_{j}\eta^{2}=...
. where
L_{j}
is
curl
or
div
. Then the argument is quite similar, if we regard
L_{j}
as a linear operator. Taking the difference, or after integrating by part in time and imposing some derivatives on it. The form of expression and their dependence on initial value has to be similar. We omit the details. For the
\bar{\partial}_{A}
estimate, in the absence of artificial viscosity on boundary, the only part needs to be mentioned is the cancellation scheme. That’s where used an extra regularity of initial data (
\|u_{0}\|\in H^{5.5}
). Then our solution can be improved in a corresponding way. Now, we don’t need to worry about the regularity of coefficient and the pressure term. For
\Delta_{g_{1}-g_{2}}
and the term involving
\phi
, the leading order term still maintain the cancellation. Hence, a similar argument in Section 4 will give us the desired result.

Appendix A
Appendix

In
[
55
]
, they proposed a physical boundary condition for nematic liquid crystal in a fixed domain with slippery condition.(See the discussion therein). Simply speaking, their condition can be written as

\displaystyle\phi^{3}=0,\quad\nabla_{N}\phi^{1}=\nabla_{N}\phi^{2}=0.

(A.1)

We don’t know whether it is still a physical condition in free boundary problem, but we can give a similar result here. Consider the stress tensor

\displaystyle S_{ij}=p(Id_{3\times 3})_{ij}+\nabla_{i}d\odot\nabla_{j}d-\frac{1}{2}|\nabla d|^{2}(Id_{3\times 3})_{ij}

(A.2)

On boundary, their requirement is roughly to say ”free slippery”:

\displaystyle pN\cdot\tau+\nabla_{N}d\odot\nabla_{\tau}d-\frac{1}{2}|\nabla d|^{2}N\cdot\tau=0.

(A.3)

In our condition, since the boundary is not fixed, we need a new constrain as

\displaystyle pN\cdot N+\nabla_{N}d\odot\nabla_{N}d-\frac{1}{2}|\nabla d|^{2}N\cdot N=-\sigma H(\eta).

(A.4)

So, if we still use their condition (
A.1
), our boundary should be written as

\displaystyle p+\nabla_{N}d^{3}\nabla_{N}d^{3}-\frac{1}{2}|\nabla d|^{2}=-\sigma H(\eta).

(A.5)

Their will be an extra term
\nabla_{N}d^{3}\nabla_{N}d^{3}
compared to our case. However, our work could still handle this situation with a slight modification. Since the boundary condition for
\phi
is still elliptic, we can obtain a similar estimate for
\phi
. We now provide a sketchy proof. Our energy estimate can start from
\|\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}(\Omega)}
and
\|\partial_{t}^{4}\phi\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}_{x}(\Omega)}
instead of
\|\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{2}(\Omega)}
and
\|\partial_{t}^{5}\phi\|_{L_{t}^{2}L^{2}_{x}(\Omega)}
. Roughly speaking, if we disregard the mollifier and the associated error analysis, the a priori energy estimate can be shown as follows:

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{5}\phi-\partial_{t}^{4}\Delta_{g}\phi=\partial_{t}^{4}(\phi|\nabla\phi|^{2})

(A.6)

Testing the
\partial_{t}^{4}\phi
, we have the standard energy estimate. For
\phi^{1}
and
\phi^{2}
, we consider

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{4}\bar{\partial}\phi-\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}\Delta\phi=\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}(\phi|\nabla\phi|^{2}).

(A.7)

Testing
\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}v\cdot\nabla\phi
, we will have a similar cancellation structure for
\phi^{1}
and
\phi^{2}
.
On the other hand, the Euler equation can be written as

\displaystyle\partial_{t}v^{j}+\nabla_{j}p=-\partial_{t}\phi\nabla_{j}\phi-\nabla_{i}\phi\nabla_{i}\nabla_{j}\phi+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{j}|\nabla_{N}\phi^{3}|^{2},

(A.8)

where
\nabla_{N}\phi^{3}=\frac{A_{i}^{3}}{|A_{.}^{3}|}A_{i}^{j}\partial_{j}\phi^{3}
. For
\phi^{1}
and
\phi^{2}
, we perform a similar cancellation as in the previous section. For
\phi^{3}
, the leading term reads

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\nabla_{i}\phi^{3}\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}(\nabla_{i}\phi^{3})\nabla_{j}\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{j}}_{\text{div }v=0,l.o.t.}-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\partial\Omega}A_{i}^{3}\partial_{3}\phi^{3}\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}(A_{i}^{3}\partial_{3}\phi^{3})A_{j}^{3}\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{j}\\
-&\underbrace{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\nabla_{N}\phi^{3}\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}\nabla_{N}\phi^{3}\nabla_{j}\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{j}}_{\text{div }v=0,l.o.t.}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\partial\Omega}A_{i}^{3}\partial_{3}\phi^{3}\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}(A_{i}^{3}\partial_{3}\phi^{3})A_{j}^{3}\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\partial}v^{j}\\
=&l.o.t.\end{aligned}

(A.9)

Thus, we can establish the uniform-
\kappa
energy estimate in a similar way, with the help of error analysis. Actually, if we don’t have the term
\frac{1}{2}\nabla|\nabla_{N}\phi^{3}|^{2}
, we can still cancel the boundary terms via harmonic heat flow. We omit the details.
